\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\log{m}}\int_{0}^{n}\left[1-\sum_{i=0}^{m}\dbinom{m}{i}\left(e^{-t}\frac{t^{2}}{n}\right)^{i}\left(1-e^{-t}\right)^{m-i}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\log{m}}\int_{0}^{n}\left[1-\left(1-e^{-t}\right)^{m}\right]\ dt

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\frac{1}{\log{m}}\int_{0}^{n}\sum_{i=1}^{m}\dbinom{m}{i}\left(e^{-t}\frac{t^{2}}{n}\right)^{i}\left(1-e^{-t}\right)^{m-i}\ dt

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\log{m}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\left[1-\left(1-e^{-t}\right)^{m}\right]\ dt

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\frac{1}{\log{m}}\int_{n}^{\infty}\left[1-\left(1-e^{-t}\right)^{m}\right]\ dt

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad-\frac{1}{\log{m}}\int_{0}^{n}\sum_{i=1}^{m}\dbinom{m}{i}\left(e^{-t}\frac{t^{2}}{n}\right)^{i}\left(1-e^{-t}\right)^{m-i}\ dt

\displaystyle:=a_{m,1}-a_{m,2}-a_{m,3}

(A.3)

We next show that
a_{m,2},a_{m,3}\longrightarrow 0
and
a_{m,1}\longrightarrow 1
as
m\to\infty
, as long as
n\geq 2m
. We start with
a_{m,3}
:

\displaystyle a_{m,3}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\log{m}}\int_{0}^{n}\sum_{i=1}^{m}\dbinom{m}{i}\left(e^{-t}\frac{t^{2}}{n}\right)^{i}\left(1-e^{-t}\right)^{m-i}\ dt

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\log{m}}\int_{0}^{n}\sum_{i=1}^{m}\dbinom{m}{i}\left(e^{-t}\frac{t^{2}}{n}\right)^{i}\ dt

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\log{m}}\sum_{i=1}^{m}\dbinom{m}{i}\int_{0}^{n}\left(e^{-t}\frac{t^{2}}{n}\right)^{i}\ dt

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\log{m}}\sum_{i=1}^{m}\dbinom{m}{i}\frac{1}{in^{i}}\int_{0}^{n}t^{2i}\ ie^{-it}\ dt

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\log{m}}\sum_{i=1}^{m}\dbinom{m}{i}\frac{1}{in^{i}}\int_{0}^{\infty}t^{2i}\ ie^{-it}\ dt

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\log{m}}\sum_{i=1}^{m}\dbinom{m}{i}\frac{1}{in^{i}}\frac{(2i)!}{i^{2i}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\log{m}}\sum_{i=1}^{m}\frac{(m-i+1)\cdots m}{in^{i}i^{i}}\frac{(i+1)\cdots(i+i)}{i^{i}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\log{m}}\sum_{i=1}^{m}\frac{(m-i+1)\cdots m}{i\left(\frac{n}{2}\right)^{i}i^{i}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\log{m}}\sum_{i=1}^{m}\left(\frac{2m}{n}\right)^{i}\frac{1}{i^{i+1}}\leq\frac{1}{\log{m}}\sum_{i=1}^{m}\frac{1}{i^{i+1}}\longrightarrow 0\,.

For the other term
a_{n,2}
:

\displaystyle\left|a_{n,2}\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\frac{1}{\log{m}}\int_{n}^{\infty}\left[1-\left(1-e^{-t}\right)^{m}\right]\ dt\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\frac{1}{\log{m}}\int_{n}^{\infty}\left[1-\sum_{i=0}^{m}\dbinom{m}{i}e^{-it}(-1)^{i}\right]\ dt\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\frac{1}{\log{m}}\int_{n}^{\infty}\sum_{i=1}^{m}\dbinom{m}{i}e^{-it}(-1)^{i+1}\ dt\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\log{m}}\sum_{i=1}^{m}\dbinom{m}{i}\int_{n}^{\infty}e^{-it}\ dt

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\log{m}}\sum_{i=1}^{m}\dbinom{m}{i}\frac{e^{-ni}}{i}

\displaystyle=\frac{2^{m}}{\log{m}}\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{e^{-nX}}{X}\mathds{1}_{X\geq 1}\right]\hskip 14.45377pt[X\sim\text{Bin}(n,p)]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2^{m}}{\log{m}}\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{e^{-2mX}}{X}\mathds{1}_{X\geq 1}\right]\hskip 14.45377pt[\because n\geq 2m]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2^{m}}{\log{m}}\mathbb{E}\left[e^{-2mX}\right]\hskip 14.45377pt[\because n\geq 2m]

\displaystyle=\frac{2^{m}}{\log{m}}\left(\frac{1}{2}e^{-2m}+\frac{1}{2}\right)^{m}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\log{m}}\left(e^{-2m}+1\right)^{m}\longrightarrow 0\,.

Now the calculation of
a_{n,1}
is similar by replacing
n
with
0
. We have:

\displaystyle a_{n,1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\log{m}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\left[1-\left(1-e^{-t}\right)^{m}\right]\ dt

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\log{m}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\sum_{i=1}^{m}\dbinom{m}{i}e^{-it}(-1)^{i+1}\ dt

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\log{m}}\sum_{i=1}^{m}\dbinom{m}{i}\frac{(-1)^{i+1}}{i}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\log{m}}\sum_{i=1}^{m}\frac{1}{i}\overset{m\to\infty}{\longrightarrow}1\,.

where the last equality follows from the representation of Harmonic number. Therefore from equation (
A.3
) we conclude:

\liminf_{m,n\to\infty}\frac{1}{\log{m}}\mathbb{E}\left[\max_{1\leq i\leq m}nZ_{i:n}\right]\geq 1\,.

This concludes the proof.

A.4
Proof of Theorem
3.6

A.4.1
Case 1:
0\leq k<\infty

We first establish the rate of convergence of
(\hat{\alpha}_{\text{init}},\hat{\beta}_{\text{init}},\hat{d})
. Towards that direction, we use the following semi-metric over the parameter space
\Theta
:

{\sf dist}(\theta_{1},\theta_{2})=\sqrt{\left(\alpha_{1}-\alpha_{2}\right)^{2}+\left(\beta_{1}-\beta_{2}\right)^{2}+\mathbb{P}\left(\text{sign}(X^{\top}d)\neq\text{sign}(X^{\top}d_{0})\right)}

The curvature of the population score function
\mathbb{M}(\theta)
around the its value at minimizer
\mathbb{M}(\theta_{0})
is obtained via the similar calculation as in in the proof of Theorem
2.1
(specifically equation (
A.5
)). Consider all
\theta\in\Theta
such that
{\sf dist}(\theta,\theta_{0})\leq\delta
where
\delta
is such that
|\alpha_{0}-\beta_{0}|>2\delta
. For such that
\theta
we have:

\displaystyle\mathbb{M}(\theta)-\mathbb{M}(\theta_{0})

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+\alpha_{0}-\alpha\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi_{i})\right)\right]\mathbb{P}\left(X^{\top}d\vee X^{\top}d_{0}\leq 0\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+\alpha_{0}-\beta\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi_{i})\right)\right]\mathbb{P}\left(X^{\top}d_{0}<0<X^{\top}d\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+\beta_{0}-\alpha\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi_{i})\right)\right]\mathbb{P}\left(X^{\top}d<0<X^{\top}d_{0}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+\beta_{0}-\beta\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi_{i})\right)\right]\mathbb{P}\left(X^{\top}d\wedge X^{\top}d_{0}>0\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{C_{k}}{2}\left[(\alpha_{0}-\alpha)^{2}\mathbb{P}\left(X^{\top}d\vee X^{\top}d_{0}\leq 0\right))+(\beta_{0}-\beta)^{2}\mathbb{P}\left(X^{\top}d\wedge X^{\top}d_{0}>0\right)\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 100.00015pt\left.+\mathbb{P}\left(\text{sign}(X^{\top}d)\neq\text{sign}(X^{\top}d_{0})\right)\left\{2(\alpha_{0}-\beta_{0}-\delta)^{2}\right\}\right]\hskip 14.45377pt[\text{Lemma }\ref{lem:huber_lower_bound}]

\displaystyle\geq C_{k}\left[(\alpha_{0}-\alpha)^{2}+(\beta_{0}-\beta)^{2}+\mathbb{P}\left(\text{sign}(X^{\top}d)\neq\text{sign}(X^{\top}d_{0})\right)|\right]

\displaystyle=C_{k}{\sf dist}^{2}(\theta,\theta_{0})\,.

(A.4)

Consistency:

We use argmin continuous mapping theorem (Theorem 3.2.2 of
[
29
]
) to establish the consistency of the initial estimator. As the parameter space is bounded, our estimates are by default tight. As the process
\mathbb{M}(\theta)-\mathbb{M}(\theta_{0})
is continuous with respect to
\theta
and has a clear minima at
\theta=\theta_{0}
all we need to show for any compact subset
K\subseteq\Theta
:

\sup_{\theta\in K}\left|\left(\mathbb{M}_{n}(\theta)-\mathbb{M}_{n}(\theta_{0})\right)-\left(\mathbb{M}(\theta)-\mathbb{M}(\theta_{0})\right)\right|=o_{p}(1)\,.

Consider a collection of functions
\mathcal{F}=\left\{f_{\theta}:\theta\in\Theta\right\}
where the individual functions
f_{\theta}(X,\xi)
is defined as:

\displaystyle f_{\theta}(X,\xi)

\displaystyle=\left(\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi+\alpha_{0}-\alpha\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi)\right)\mathds{1}_{X^{\top}d\vee X^{\top}d_{0}\leq 0}

\displaystyle\qquad\left(\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi+\alpha_{0}-\beta\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi)\right)\mathds{1}_{X^{\top}d_{0}\leq 0<X^{\top}d}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\left(\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi+\beta_{0}-\alpha\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi)\right)\mathds{1}_{X^{\top}d\leq 0<X^{\top}d_{0}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\left(\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi+\beta_{0}-\beta\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi)\right)\mathds{1}_{X^{\top}d\wedge X^{\top}d_{0}>0}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{i=1}^{4}g^{i}_{\theta}(\xi)h^{i}_{\theta}(X)

with:

\displaystyle g^{1}_{\theta}(\xi)=\left(\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi+\alpha_{0}-\alpha\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi)\right),

\displaystyle h^{1}_{\theta}(X)=\mathds{1}_{X^{\top}d\vee X^{\top}d_{0}\leq 0}\,,

\displaystyle g^{2}_{\theta}(\xi)=\left(H_{k}\left(\xi+\alpha_{0}-\beta\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi)\right),

\displaystyle h^{2}_{\theta}(X)=\mathds{1}_{X^{\top}d_{0}\leq 0<X^{\top}d}\,,

\displaystyle g^{3}_{\theta}(\xi)=\left(\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi+\beta_{0}-\alpha\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi)\right),

\displaystyle h^{3}_{\theta}(X)=\mathds{1}_{X^{\top}d\leq 0<X^{\top}d_{0}}\,,

\displaystyle g^{4}_{\theta}(\xi)=\left(\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi+\beta_{0}-\beta\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi)\right),

\displaystyle h^{4}_{\theta}(X)=\mathds{1}_{X^{\top}d\wedge X^{\top}d_{0}>0}\,.

As the Huber function
H_{k}
is Lipschitz with Lipschitz constant
k
, our criterion function
\tilde{H}_{k}
is Lipschitz with Lipschitz constant
(k+1)
. As our parameter space is compact, the functions
\{g^{i}_{\theta},h^{i}_{\theta}\}_{1\leq i\leq 4}
are uniformly bounded, and has constant envelope, say
F
. That the functions
\{g^{i}_{\theta}\}_{\theta\in\Theta}
for
i=1,2,3,4
has finite VC dimension
v
(i.e. does not grow with
n
or
p
) is immediate. On the other hands, as all the
p
-dimensional hyperplanes passing through origin has VC dimension
p
. Hence the functions
\{h^{i}_{\theta}\}_{\theta\in\Theta}
has VC dimension
p
. Define
\mathcal{F}_{g,i}=\{g^{i}_{\theta}:\theta\in\Theta\}
for
1\leq i\leq 4
and
\mathcal{F}_{h,i}=\{h_{\theta}^{i}:\theta\in\Theta\}
for
1\leq i\leq 4
. Combining these we obtain:

\displaystyle\sup_{Q}N({\epsilon}\|F\|_{Q,1},\mathcal{F},L_{1}(Q))

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{Q}N\left({\epsilon}\|F\|_{Q,1},\sum_{i=1}^{4}\mathcal{F}_{g,i}\mathcal{F}_{h,i},L_{1}(Q)\right)

\displaystyle\leq\Pi_{i=1}^{4}\sup_{Q}N({\epsilon}\|F\|_{Q,1},\mathcal{F}_{g,i}\mathcal{F}_{h,i},L_{1}(Q))

\displaystyle\leq\Pi_{i=1}^{4}KVC(\mathcal{F}_{g,i})VC(\mathcal{F}_{h,i})(16e)^{VC(\mathcal{F}_{g,i})+VC(\mathcal{F}_{h,i})}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\left(\frac{1}{{\epsilon}}\right)^{(VC(\mathcal{F}_{g,i})+VC(\mathcal{F}_{h,i})-2)}

\displaystyle\leq\Pi_{i=1}^{4}Kvp(16e)^{v+p}\left(\frac{1}{{\epsilon}}\right)^{(v+p-2)}

\displaystyle=K^{4}(16e)^{4(v+p)}\left(\frac{1}{{\epsilon}}\right)^{4(v+p-2)}

\displaystyle=K^{4}\left(\frac{16e}{{\epsilon}}\right)^{4(v+p)}

This along with the fact
p/n\to 0
implies:

\frac{1}{n}\log{\left(\sup_{Q}N({\epsilon}\|F\|_{Q,1},\mathcal{F},L_{1}(Q)))\right)}\to 0\,.

Therefore
\mathcal{F}
is Glivenko-Cantelli class of functions and using Theorem 2.4.3 of
[
29
]
we conclude that:

\displaystyle\sup_{\theta\in K}\left|\left(\mathbb{M}_{n}(\theta)-\mathbb{M}_{n}(\theta_{0})\right)-\left(\mathbb{M}(\theta)-\mathbb{M}(\theta_{0})\right)\right|

\displaystyle=\left\|\left(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P\right)\right\|_{\mathcal{F}}=o_{p}(1)\,.

This establishes the consistency of
(\hat{\alpha}_{\text{init}},\hat{\beta}_{\text{init}},\hat{d})
.

Rate of convergence of initial estimators:

So far we have established the quadratic curvature of
\mathbb{M}(\theta)
around its unique minimizer
\theta_{0}
and also the consistency of
\hat{\theta}_{\text{init}}=(\hat{\alpha}_{\text{init}},\hat{\beta}_{\text{init}},\hat{d})
. In this section we show that:

\sqrt{\frac{n}{p}}\left(\log{\frac{n}{p}}\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\sf dist}(\hat{\theta}_{\text{init}},\theta_{0})=O_{p}(1)

which (along with Assumption
3.5
) implies:

\frac{n}{p}\left(\log{\frac{n}{p}}\right)^{-1}\left[\left(\hat{\alpha}_{\text{init}}-\alpha_{0}\right)^{2}+\left(\hat{\beta}_{\text{init}}-\beta_{0}\right)^{2}+\left\|\hat{d}-d_{0}\right\|\right]=O_{p}(1)\,.

To establish the rate, all is left to do is to find a bound on the modulus of continuity of the empirical process
\mathbb{M}_{n}(\theta)
, i.e we need to find
\phi_{n}(\delta)
such that:

\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{d(\theta,\theta_{0})\leq\delta}\left|(\mathbb{M}_{n}-\mathbb{M})(\theta_{0})-(\mathbb{M}_{n}-\mathbb{M})(\theta)\right|\right]\leq\frac{\phi_{n}(\delta)}{\sqrt{n}}\,.

(A.5)

Towards that end, define a local collection of functions
\mathcal{F}_{\delta}=\left\{f_{\theta}:d(\theta,\theta_{0})\leq\delta\right\}
. Note that when
d(\theta,\theta_{0})\leq\delta
we have:

\max\left\{|\alpha-\alpha_{0}|,|\beta-\beta_{0}|,\sqrt{\mathbb{P}\left(\text{sign}(X^{\top}d)\neq\text{sign}(X^{\top}d_{0})\right)}\right\}\leq\delta\,.

For any such
\theta
we have:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[f_{\theta}(X,\xi)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left(\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi+\alpha_{0}-\alpha\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi)\right)^{2}\mathbb{P}\left(X^{\top}d\vee X^{\top}d_{0}\leq 0\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\mathbb{E}\left(\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi+\alpha_{0}-\beta\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi)\right)^{2}\mathbb{P}\left(X^{\top}d_{0}\leq 0<X^{\top}d\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\mathbb{E}\left(\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi+\beta_{0}-\alpha\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi)\right)^{2}\mathbb{P}\left(X^{\top}d\leq 0<X^{\top}d_{0}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\mathbb{E}\left(\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi+\beta_{0}-\beta\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi)\right)^{2}\mathbb{P}\left(X^{\top}d\wedge X^{\top}d_{0}>0\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim C_{k}\left[\delta^{2}+\mathbb{P}\left(\text{sign}(X^{\top}d)\neq\text{sign}(X^{\top}d_{0})\right)\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim C_{k}\delta^{2}

Hence applying Theorem 8.7 of
[
26
]
we conclude:

\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{d(\theta,\theta_{0})\leq\delta}\left|(\mathbb{M}_{n}-\mathbb{M})(\theta_{0})-(\mathbb{M}_{n}-\mathbb{M})(\theta)\right|\right]\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}\delta\sqrt{\log{\frac{1}{\delta}}}\vee\frac{p}{n}\log{\frac{1}{\delta}}

Therefore a valid choice of
\phi_{n}
in equation (
A.5
) is:

\phi_{n}(\delta)=\sqrt{p}\delta\sqrt{\log{\frac{1}{\delta}}}\vee\frac{p}{\sqrt{n}}\log{\frac{1}{\delta}}\,.

Using this
\phi_{n}
in Theorem 3.4.1 of
[
29
]
we conclude that:

\sqrt{\frac{n}{p}}\left(\log{\frac{n}{p}}\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}d(\hat{\theta}_{\text{init}},\theta_{0})=O_{p}(1)\,.

Finally, as the function class under consideration
\mathcal{F}=\{f_{\theta}:\theta\in\Omega\times S^{p-1}\}
is uniformly bounded, an application of Theorem 2 of
[
23
]
yields:

\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{n}{p}\left(\log{\frac{n}{p}}\right)^{-1}d(\hat{\theta}_{\text{init}},\theta_{0})\geq t\right)\leq C_{k}e^{-c_{k}t}

(A.6)

for some constants
C_{k},c_{k}>0
which depends on
k
. This in particular implies that:

\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{n}{p}\left(\log{\frac{n}{p}}\right)^{-1}\mathbb{P}\left(\text{sign}(X^{\top}\hat{d})\neq\text{sign}(X^{\top}d_{0})\right)\right]\leq C_{k}

(A.7)

for some constant
C_{k}>0
depends on
k
.

Rate of convergence of the final estimators:

We now present the proof that the rate of convergence of the final estimator. The proof for
\hat{\alpha}
and
\hat{\beta}
are similar and therefore we only the present the proof for
\hat{\alpha}
. Before delving into the technical details, we introduce some notation:

\displaystyle\mathbb{M}_{n}(\alpha,d)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\tilde{H}_{k}(Y_{i}-\alpha)\mathds{1}_{X_{i}^{\top}d\leq 0}

\displaystyle\mathbb{R}_{n}(\alpha,d_{1},d_{2})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\tilde{H}_{k}(Y_{i}-\alpha)\left(\mathds{1}_{X_{i}^{\top}d_{1}\leq 0}-\mathds{1}_{X_{i}^{\top}d_{2}\leq 0}\right)

\displaystyle\mathbb{M}(\alpha,d)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[\mathbb{M}_{n}(\alpha,d)]=\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{H}_{k}(Y-\alpha)\mathds{1}_{X^{\top}d\leq 0}\right]\,.

From Lemma
A.8
we have for all
|\alpha-\alpha_{0}|\leq\eta
(for some small enough
\eta>0
):

\mathbb{M}(\alpha,d_{0})-\mathbb{M}(\alpha_{0},d_{0})\geq C_{k}(\alpha-\alpha_{0})^{2}

In terms of the processes introduced above, we can write our final estimator
\hat{\alpha}
as:

\displaystyle\hat{\alpha}

\displaystyle={\arg\min}_{\alpha}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\tilde{H}_{k}(Y_{i}-\alpha)\mathds{1}_{X_{i}^{\top}\hat{d}\leq 0}

\displaystyle={\arg\min}_{\alpha}\mathbb{M}_{n}(\alpha,\hat{d})

\displaystyle={\arg\min}_{\alpha}\left[\mathbb{M}_{n}(\alpha,d_{0})+\mathbb{R}_{n}(\alpha,\hat{d},d_{0})\right]

(A.8)

Consistency of the above estimator follows from the similar calculation as of its previous incarnation, hence we skip it here for brevity.
The remainder term
\mathbb{R}_{n}
can be bounded as:

\displaystyle\sup_{\alpha:|\alpha-\alpha_{0}|\leq\delta}\left|\mathbb{R}_{n}(\alpha,\hat{d},d_{0})-\mathbb{R}_{n}(\alpha_{0},\hat{d},d_{0})\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{k\delta}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathds{1}_{\text{sign}(X_{i}^{\top}\hat{d})\neq\text{sign}(X_{i}^{\top}d_{0})}

\displaystyle=k\delta\mathbb{P}_{n}f_{\hat{d}}

\displaystyle=k\delta\left[(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P)f_{\hat{d}}+Pf_{\hat{d}}\right]

Fix
{\epsilon}>0
. Previously, we have established that:

Pf_{\hat{d}}=O_{p}\left(\frac{p}{n}\log{\frac{n}{p}}\right)\,.

Therefore we can find
t_{0}
such that for all
t>t_{0}
:

\mathbb{P}\left(Pf_{\hat{d}}>t_{0}\frac{p}{n}\log{\frac{n}{p}}\right)\leq{\epsilon}\,.

Next we bound the fluctuation of the centered empirical process.

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{n}{p}\left(\log{\frac{n}{p}}\right)^{-1}(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P)f_{\hat{d}}>t\right)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{n}{p}\left(\log{\frac{n}{p}}\right)^{-1}(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P)f_{\hat{d}}>t,Pf_{\hat{d}}\leq t_{0}\frac{p}{n}\log{\frac{n}{p}}\right)+\mathbb{P}\left(Pf_{\hat{d}}>t_{0}\frac{p}{n}\log{\frac{n}{p}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left(\sup_{d:Pf_{d}\leq t_{0}\frac{p}{n}\log{\frac{n}{p}}}\frac{n}{p}\left(\log{\frac{n}{p}}\right)^{-1}\left\|(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P)f_{d}\right\|>t\right)+{\epsilon}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{n}{p}\left(\log{\frac{n}{p}}\right)^{-1}\times\frac{1}{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{d:Pf_{d}\leq t_{0}\frac{p}{n}\log{\frac{n}{p}}}\left\|(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P)f_{d}\right\|\right]+{\epsilon}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{n}{p}\left(\log{\frac{n}{p}}\right)^{-1}\times\frac{\sqrt{t_{0}}\frac{p}{n}\sqrt{\log{\frac{n}{p}}\log{\frac{n}{t_{0}p\log{(n/p)}}}}\vee\frac{p}{n}\log{\frac{n}{t_{0}p\log{(n/p)}}}}{t}+{\epsilon}\hskip 14.45377pt[\text{Theorem 8.7 of \cite[cite]{[\@@bibref{Number}{sen2018gentle}{}{}]}}]

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{t_{0}}}{t}\sqrt{\frac{\log{(n/p)}-\log{\left(t_{0}\log{(n/p)}\right)}}{\log{(n/p)}}}\ \vee\frac{1}{t}\frac{\log{(n/p)}-\log{\left(t_{0}\log{(n/p)}\right)}}{\log{(n/p)}}+{\epsilon}

Therefore we have:

\limsup_{n\to\infty}\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{n}{p}\left(\log{\frac{n}{p}}\right)^{-1}(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P)f_{\hat{d}}>t\right)\leq\frac{\sqrt{t_{0}}}{t}\vee\frac{1}{t}+{\epsilon}\,,

which implies that for any fixed
{\epsilon}>0
, we can
t
large enough to ensure that:

\limsup_{n\to\infty}\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{n}{p}\left(\log{\frac{n}{p}}\right)^{-1}(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P)f_{\hat{d}}>t\right)\leq 2{\epsilon}

Hence we have:

\sup_{|\alpha-\alpha_{0}|\leq\delta}\left|\mathbb{R}_{n}(\alpha,\hat{d},d_{0})-\mathbb{R}_{n}(\alpha_{0},\hat{d},d_{0})\right|=\delta\times O_{p}\left(\frac{p}{n}\log{\frac{n}{p}}\right)\,.

Again fix
{\epsilon}>0
and choose
t_{0}>0
such that:

\limsup_{n\to\infty}\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{n}{p}\left(\log{\frac{n}{p}}\right)^{-1}\sup_{|\alpha-\alpha_{0}|\leq\delta}\left|\mathbb{R}_{n}(\alpha,\hat{d},d_{0})-\mathbb{R}_{n}(\alpha_{0},\hat{d},d_{0})\right|\geq\delta t_{0}\right)\leq{\epsilon}\,.

Also note that this
t_{0}
only depends on
{\epsilon}
, not
\delta
. Henceforth define
r_{n}=\sqrt{n}\wedge(n/p)(\log{(n/p)})^{-1}
, the desired rate of convergence for the second stage estimator for notational simplicity. Further, by an application of Lemma 2.14.1 of
[
29
]
we have for any
\delta>0
:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{|\alpha-\alpha_{0}|\leq\delta}\left|(\mathbb{M}_{n}-\mathbb{M})(\alpha,d_{0})-(\mathbb{M}_{n}-\mathbb{M})(\alpha_{0},d_{0})\right|\right]\lesssim\frac{\delta}{\sqrt{n}}\,.

(A.9)

Using a shelling type of argument, we have for any
t>0
:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(r_{n}|\hat{\alpha}-\alpha_{0}|>t\right)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left(r_{n}|\hat{\alpha}-\alpha_{0}|>t,|\hat{\alpha}-\alpha_{0}|\leq\eta\right)+\mathbb{P}\left(|\hat{\alpha}-\alpha_{0}|>\eta\right)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left(r_{n}|\hat{\alpha}-\alpha_{0}|>t,|\hat{\alpha}-\alpha_{0}|\leq\eta\right)+{\epsilon}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left(\sup_{\alpha:tr_{n}^{-1}<|\alpha-\alpha_{0}|\leq\eta}\left\{\mathbb{M}_{n}(\alpha_{0},d_{0})+\mathbb{R}_{n}(\alpha_{0},\hat{d},d_{0})-\mathbb{M}_{n}(\alpha,d_{0})-\mathbb{R}_{n}(\alpha,\hat{d},d_{0})\right\}\geq 0\right)+{\epsilon}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left(\sup_{\alpha:tr_{n}^{-1}<|\alpha-\alpha_{0}|\leq\eta}\left\{\mathbb{M}_{n}(\alpha_{0},d_{0})+\mathbb{R}_{n}(\alpha_{0},\hat{d},d_{0})-\mathbb{M}_{n}(\alpha,d_{0})-\mathbb{R}_{n}(\alpha,\hat{d},d_{0})\right\}\geq 0,\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 200.0003pt\left.\mathbb{P}_{n}f_{\hat{d}}\leq t_{0}\frac{p}{n}\log{\frac{n}{p}}\right)+\mathbb{P}\left(\mathbb{P}_{n}f_{\hat{d}}>t_{0}\frac{p}{n}\log{\frac{n}{p}}\right)+{\epsilon}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left(\sup_{\alpha:tr_{n}^{-1}<|\alpha-\alpha_{0}|\leq\eta}\left\{\mathbb{M}_{n}(\alpha_{0},d_{0})+\mathbb{R}_{n}(\alpha_{0},\hat{d},d_{0})-\mathbb{M}_{n}(\alpha,d_{0})-\mathbb{R}_{n}(\alpha,\hat{d},d_{0})\right\}\geq 0,\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 250.00038pt\left.\mathbb{P}_{n}f_{\hat{d}}\leq t_{0}\frac{p}{n}\log{\frac{n}{p}}\right)+2{\epsilon}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=1}^{\log_{2}{(\eta r_{n}/t)}}\mathbb{P}\left(\sup_{\alpha:2^{j-1}tr_{n}^{-1}<|\alpha-\alpha_{0}|\leq 2^{j}tr_{n}^{-1}}\left\{\mathbb{M}_{n}(\alpha_{0},d_{0})+\mathbb{R}_{n}(\alpha_{0},\hat{d},d_{0})\right.\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\left.\left.-\mathbb{M}_{n}(\alpha,d_{0})-\mathbb{R}_{n}(\alpha,\hat{d},d_{0})\right\}\geq 0,\mathbb{P}_{n}f_{\hat{d}}\leq t_{0}\frac{p}{n}\log{\frac{n}{p}}\right)+2{\epsilon}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=1}^{\log_{2}{(\eta r_{n}/t)}}\mathbb{P}\left(\sup_{\alpha:2^{j-1}tr_{n}^{-1}<|\alpha-\alpha_{0}|\leq 2^{j}tr_{n}^{-1}}\left\{\mathbb{M}_{n}(\alpha_{0},d_{0})-\mathbb{M}_{n}(\alpha,d_{0})\right\}\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\left.+\sup_{2^{j-1}tr_{n}^{-1}<|\alpha-\alpha_{0}|\leq 2^{j}tr_{n}^{-1}}\left|\mathbb{R}_{n}(\alpha,\hat{d},d_{0})-\mathbb{R}_{n}(\alpha_{0},\hat{d},d_{0})\right|\geq 0,\mathbb{P}_{n}f_{\hat{d}}\leq t_{0}\frac{p}{n}\log{\frac{n}{p}}\right)+2{\epsilon}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=1}^{\log_{2}{(\eta r_{n}/t)}}\mathbb{P}\left(\sup_{\alpha:2^{j-1}tr_{n}^{-1}<|\alpha-\alpha_{0}|\leq 2^{j}tr_{n}^{-1}}\left\{\mathbb{M}_{n}(\alpha_{0},d_{0})-\mathbb{M}_{n}(\alpha,d_{0})\right\}+2^{j}tr_{n}^{-1}t_{0}\frac{p}{n}\log{\frac{n}{p}}\geq 0\right)+2{\epsilon}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=1}^{\log_{2}{(\eta r_{n}/t)}}\mathbb{P}\left(\sup_{\alpha:2^{j-1}tr_{n}^{-1}<|\alpha-\alpha_{0}|\leq 2^{j}tr_{n}^{-1}}\left\{\mathbb{M}_{n}(\alpha_{0},d_{0})-\mathbb{M}_{n}(\alpha,d_{0})\right\}+2^{j}tr_{n}^{-1}t_{0}\frac{p}{n}\log{\frac{n}{p}}\geq 0\right)+2{\epsilon}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=1}^{\log_{2}{(\eta r_{n}/t)}}\mathbb{P}\left(\sup_{\alpha:2^{j-1}tr_{n}^{-1}<|\alpha-\alpha_{0}|\leq 2^{j}tr_{n}^{-1}}\left\{(\mathbb{M}_{n}-\mathbb{M})(\alpha_{0},d_{0})-(\mathbb{M}_{n}-\mathbb{M})(\alpha,d_{0})\right\}\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\left.+2^{j}tr_{n}^{-1}t_{0}\frac{p}{n}\log{\frac{n}{p}}\geq\inf_{\alpha:2^{j-1}tr_{n}^{-1}<|\alpha-\alpha_{0}|\leq 2^{j}tr_{n}^{-1}}\left(\mathbb{M}(\alpha)-\mathbb{M}(\alpha_{0})\right)\right)+2{\epsilon}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=1}^{\log_{2}{(\eta r_{n}/t)}}\frac{\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{2^{j-1}tr_{n}^{-1}<|\alpha-\alpha_{0}|\leq 2^{j}tr_{n}^{-1}}\left\{(\mathbb{M}_{n}-\mathbb{M})(\alpha_{0},d_{0})-(\mathbb{M}_{n}-\mathbb{M})(\alpha,d_{0})\right\}\right]+2^{j}tr_{n}^{-1}t_{0}\frac{p}{n}\log{\frac{n}{p}}}{\inf_{\alpha:2^{j-1}tr_{n}^{-1}<|\alpha-\alpha_{0}|\leq 2^{j}tr_{n}^{-1}}\left(\mathbb{M}(\alpha)-\mathbb{M}(\alpha_{0})\right)}+2{\epsilon}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=1}^{\log_{2}{(\eta r_{n}/t)}}\frac{\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{|\alpha-\alpha_{0}|\leq 2^{j}tr_{n}^{-1}}\left\{(\mathbb{M}_{n}-\mathbb{M})(\alpha_{0},d_{0})-(\mathbb{M}_{n}-\mathbb{M})(\alpha,d_{0})\right\}\right]+2^{j}tr_{n}^{-1}t_{0}\frac{p}{n}\log{\frac{n}{p}}}{\inf_{\alpha:|\alpha-\alpha_{0}|\geq 2^{j-1}tr_{n}^{-1}}\left(\mathbb{M}(\alpha)-\mathbb{M}(\alpha_{0})\right)}+2{\epsilon}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=1}^{\log_{2}{(\eta r_{n}/t)}}\frac{\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{|\alpha-\alpha_{0}|\leq 2^{j}tr_{n}^{-1}}\left\{(\mathbb{M}_{n}-\mathbb{M})(\alpha_{0},d_{0})-(\mathbb{M}_{n}-\mathbb{M})(\alpha,d_{0})\right\}\right]+2^{j}tt_{0}r_{n}^{-2}}{\inf_{\alpha:|\alpha-\alpha_{0}|\geq 2^{j-1}tr_{n}^{-1}}\left(\mathbb{M}(\alpha)-\mathbb{M}(\alpha_{0})\right)}+2{\epsilon}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=1}^{\log_{2}{(\eta r_{n}/t)}}\frac{\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{|\alpha-\alpha_{0}|\leq 2^{j}tr_{n}^{-1}}\left\{(\mathbb{M}_{n}-\mathbb{M})(\alpha_{0},d_{0})-(\mathbb{M}_{n}-\mathbb{M})(\alpha,d_{0})\right\}\right]+2^{j}tt_{0}r_{n}^{-2}}{2^{2(j-1)}t^{2}r_{n}^{-2}}+2{\epsilon}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=1}^{\log_{2}{(\eta r_{n}/t)}}\frac{\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{|\alpha-\alpha_{0}|\leq 2^{j}tr_{n}^{-1}}\left\{(\mathbb{M}_{n}-\mathbb{M})(\alpha_{0},d_{0})-(\mathbb{M}_{n}-\mathbb{M})(\alpha,d_{0})\right\}\right]}{2^{2(j-1)}t^{2}r_{n}^{-2}}+\frac{t_{0}}{t}+2{\epsilon}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=1}^{\log_{2}{(\eta r_{n}/t)}}\frac{2^{j}tr_{n}^{-1}}{\sqrt{n}2^{2(j-1)}t^{2}r_{n}^{-2}}+\frac{t_{0}}{t}+2{\epsilon}\hskip 14.45377pt[\text{From equation \eqref{eq:acc_moc}}]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{t}+\frac{t_{0}}{t}+2{\epsilon}\,.

Taking
t
large enough we conclude:

\limsup_{n\to\infty}\mathbb{P}\left(r_{n}|\hat{\alpha}-\alpha_{0}|>t\right)\leq 3{\epsilon}

This completes the proof.

A.4.2
Case 2:
k=\infty
, i.e. squared error loss

The proof for
k=\infty
is similar to that of
0\leq k<\infty
, the only difference is that the collection of functions
\mathcal{F}
defined in the proof of the previous part is no longer bounded. Hence we need to modify some parts of the proof carefully to take care of that.

Consistency:

Consider the same function class
\mathcal{F}
as in paragraph
A.4.1
. Note that now any individual function
f_{\theta}
is:

\displaystyle f_{\theta}(X,\xi)

\displaystyle=\left(\xi(\alpha_{0}-\alpha)+\frac{1}{2}(\alpha_{0}-\alpha)^{2}\right)\mathds{1}_{X^{\top}d\vee X^{\top}d_{0}\leq 0}

\displaystyle\qquad\left(\xi(\alpha_{0}-\beta)+\frac{1}{2}(\alpha_{0}-\beta)^{2}\right)\mathds{1}_{X^{\top}d_{0}\leq 0<X^{\top}d}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\left(\xi(\beta_{0}-\alpha)+\frac{1}{2}(\beta_{0}-\alpha)^{2}\right)\mathds{1}_{X^{\top}d\leq 0<X^{\top}d_{0}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\left(\xi(\beta_{0}-\beta)+\frac{1}{2}(\beta_{0}-\beta)^{2}\right)\mathds{1}_{X^{\top}d\wedge X^{\top}d_{0}>0}

The envelope function
F
of
\mathcal{F}
is as follows:

\displaystyle\sup_{\theta\in\Theta}\left|f_{\theta}(X,\xi)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{(\alpha,\beta)\in\Omega}\left[|\xi|\max\left\{\left|\alpha-\alpha_{0}\right|,\left|\alpha-\beta_{0}\right|,\left|\beta-\alpha_{0}\right|,\left|\beta-\beta_{0}\right|\right\}\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\left.\frac{1}{2}\left(\max\left\{\left|\alpha-\alpha_{0}\right|,\left|\alpha-\beta_{0}\right|,\left|\beta-\alpha_{0}\right|,\left|\beta-\beta_{0}\right|\right\}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq C|\xi|+\frac{C^{2}}{2}:=F(X,\xi)

The envelope function is integrable and following same analysis as of paragraph
A.4.1
we conclude:

\sup_{Q}N\left({\epsilon}\left\|F\right\|_{Q,1},\mathcal{F},L_{1}(Q)\right)\leq K^{4}\left(\frac{16e}{{\epsilon}}\right)^{v+p}\,.

Hence
\mathcal{F}
is a Glivenko-Cantelli class of functions and consistency follows from Theorem 2.4.3 of
[
29
]
.

Rate of convergence of the initial estimate:

To control the modulus of continuity, we can no longer apply Theorem 8.7 of
[
26
]
directly here as the functions are not uniformly bounded. Here we use the following modified version of Theorem 1 of
[
15
]
:

Proposition A.7
.

Suppose
\{\xi_{1},\dots,\xi_{n}\}
are independent of random variables of
\{X_{1},\dots,X_{n}\}
and moreover
\{X_{1},\dots,X_{n}\}
are permutation invariant. Assume further that there exists a non-decreasing concave function
\varphi_{n}:\mathbb{R}_{+}\to\mathbb{R}_{+}
with
\varphi_{n}(0)=0
and constant
b_{n}>0
such that for
1\leq k\leq n
:

\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{k}{\epsilon}_{i}f(X_{i})\right\|_{\mathcal{F}}\leq\varphi_{n}(k)+b_{n}

for some i.i.d Rademacher random variables
{\epsilon}_{1},\dots,{\epsilon}_{n}
. Then we have:

\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\xi_{i}f(X_{i})\right\|_{\mathcal{F}}\leq 4\int_{0}^{\infty}\varphi_{n}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\xi_{i}\right|>t\right)\right)\ dt+2b_{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\left|\xi_{i}\right|\right]\,.

The proof of this proposition can be found in the Appendix
B
. To apply the above proposition, we define

\displaystyle f_{\theta}(X_{i},\xi_{i})

\displaystyle=\xi_{i}f_{\theta,1}(X_{i})+f_{\theta,2}(X_{i})

where:

\displaystyle f_{\theta,1}(X_{i})

\displaystyle=(\alpha_{0}-\alpha)\mathds{1}_{X^{\top}d\vee X^{\top}d_{0}\leq 0}+(\alpha_{0}-\beta)\mathds{1}_{X^{\top}d_{0}\leq 0<X^{\top}d}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+(\beta_{0}-\alpha)\mathds{1}_{X^{\top}d\leq 0<X^{\top}d_{0}}+(\beta_{0}-\beta)\mathds{1}_{X^{\top}d\wedge X^{\top}d_{0}>0}

\displaystyle f_{\theta_{2}}(X_{i})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}(\alpha_{0}-\alpha)^{2}\mathds{1}_{X^{\top}d\vee X^{\top}d_{0}\leq 0}+\frac{1}{2}(\alpha_{0}-\beta)^{2}\mathds{1}_{X^{\top}d_{0}\leq 0<X^{\top}d}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{2}(\beta_{0}-\alpha)^{2}\mathds{1}_{X^{\top}d\leq 0<X^{\top}d_{0}}+\frac{1}{2}(\beta_{0}-\beta)^{2}\mathds{1}_{X^{\top}d\wedge X^{\top}d_{0}>0}

Both the collections
\mathcal{F}_{1}=\{f_{\theta,1}:d(\theta,\theta_{0})\leq\delta\}
and
\mathcal{F}_{2}=\{f_{\theta,2}:d(\theta,\theta_{0})\leq\delta\}
are uniformly bounded with VC dimension of the order
p
. It is also immediate that
Pf_{\theta,j}^{2}\lesssim\delta^{2}
for all
\theta:d(\theta,\theta_{0})\leq\delta
, for
j\in\{1,2\}
. Hence we have from Theorem 8.7 of
[
26
]
for any
1\leq k\leq n
and
{\epsilon}_{1},\dots.{\epsilon}_{n}
i.i.d Rademacher random variables:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{d(\theta,\theta_{0})\leq\delta}\left|\sum_{i=1}^{k}{\epsilon}_{i}f_{\theta,j}(X_{i})\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq L\left(\delta\sqrt{k}\sqrt{p\log{\frac{AU}{\delta}}}+pU\log{\frac{AU}{\delta}}\right)

(A.10)

\displaystyle:=\varphi_{n}(k)+b_{n}

for some constants
L>0,A>e^{2}
and
U
is the uniform bounds on the individual functions and
\varphi_{n}(k)=L\delta\sqrt{k}\sqrt{p\log{\left(AU/\delta\right)}}
and
b_{n}=pU\log{(AU/\delta)}
. Therefore using Proposition
A.7
:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{k}\xi_{i}f(X_{i})\right\|_{\mathcal{F}_{1}}

\displaystyle\leq 4\int_{0}^{\infty}\varphi_{n}\left(n\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\xi_{1}\right|>t\right)\right)\ dt+2b_{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\left|\xi_{i}\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq 4L\delta\sqrt{n}\sqrt{p\log{\left(\frac{AU}{\delta}\right)}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\sqrt{\mathbb{P}(|\xi_{1}|>t)}\ dt+2pU\log{\left(\frac{AU}{\delta}\right)}\mathbb{E}\left[\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\left|\xi_{i}\right|\right]

\displaystyle=4L\left\|\xi\right\|_{2,1}\delta\sqrt{n}\sqrt{p\log{\left(\frac{AU}{\delta}\right)}}+2pU\log{\left(\frac{AU}{\delta}\right)}\mathbb{E}\left[\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\left|\xi_{i}\right|\right]

(A.11)

For the collection
\mathcal{F}_{2}
we can directly use equation (
A.10
) for
k=n
we obtain:

\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\left(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P\right)f_{\theta,2}\right\|_{\mathcal{F}_{2}}\right]\leq L\left(\frac{\delta}{\sqrt{n}}\sqrt{p\log{\frac{AU}{\delta}}}+\frac{pU}{n}\log{\frac{AU}{\delta}}\right)

(A.12)

Therefore combining equation (
A.4.2
) and (
A.12
) we conclude:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{d(\theta,\theta_{0})\leq\delta}\left|\left(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P\right)f_{\theta}\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq L(4\left\|\xi\right\|_{2,1}+1)\frac{\delta}{\sqrt{n}}\sqrt{p\log{\frac{AU}{\delta}}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{pU}{n}\log{\frac{AU}{\delta}}\left(1+2\mathbb{E}\left[\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\left|\xi_{i}\right|\right]\right)

(A.13)

Ignoring constants (as they won’t effect the rate of convergence) we can take
\phi_{n}(\delta)
is Theorem 3.4.1 of
[
29
]
as:

\phi_{n}(\delta)=\delta\sqrt{p\log{\frac{1}{\delta}}}\vee\frac{p}{\sqrt{n}}\log{\frac{1}{\delta}}\mathbb{E}\left[\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\left|\xi_{i}\right|\right]\,.

Finally solving the equation the equation
r_{n}^{2}\phi_{n}(1/r_{n})\leq\sqrt{n}
we conclude the rate of convergence.

Rate of convergence of the final estimators:

The calculation is exactly same as in Paragraph
A.4.1
and hence skipped.

A.5
Proof of Theorem
2.1

To establish the rate of convergence and the asymptotic distribution of the change point estimators obtained via Huber loss, we first need to establish a curvature of the population loss function around its unique minimizer. The following lemma is imperative to that end:

Lemma A.8
.

If
\xi
follows a symmetric distribution around the origin with with continuous density
f_{\xi}
satisfying
f_{\xi}(0)>0
, then for any
k>0,|\mu|<2k
, we have:

\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi+\mu)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi)\right]\geq\frac{\mu^{2}}{2}\mathbb{P}\left(-k\leq\xi\leq k-\mu\right)\geq\frac{\mu^{2}}{2}\mathbb{P}\left(-k\leq\xi\leq 0\right)\,.

For
k=0
, if we choose
\delta
such that for all
|x|\leq\delta
,
f_{\xi}(x)\geq f_{\xi}(0)/2
, then we have for
|\mu|\leq\delta
:

\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi+\mu)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi)\right]\geq\frac{\mu^{2}}{2}f_{\xi}(0)\,.

The proof of the above lemma can be found in Appendix
B
. Now set
\delta>0
such that
\beta_{0}+\delta<\alpha_{0}
. Define the empirical stochastic process
\mathbb{M}_{n}(\theta)
as:

\displaystyle\mathbb{M}_{n}(\theta)\equiv\mathbb{M}_{n}(\alpha,\beta,d)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\tilde{H}_{k}\left(Y_{i}-\alpha\mathds{1}_{X_{i}\leq d}-\beta\mathds{1}_{X_{i}>d}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+\alpha_{0}\mathds{1}_{X_{i}\leq d_{0}}+\beta_{0}\mathds{1}_{X_{i}>d_{0}}-\alpha\mathds{1}_{X_{i}\leq d}-\beta\mathds{1}_{X_{i}>d}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+\alpha_{0}-\alpha\right)\mathds{1}_{X_{i}\leq d_{0}\wedge d}+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+\alpha_{0}-\beta\right)\mathds{1}_{d<X_{i}\leq d_{0}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+\beta_{0}-\alpha\right)\mathds{1}_{d_{0}<X_{i}\leq d}+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+\beta_{0}-\beta\right)\mathds{1}_{X_{i}>d\vee d_{0}}

This implies the centred empirical stochastic process is:

\displaystyle\mathbb{M}_{n}(\theta)-\mathbb{M}_{n}(\theta_{0})

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{M}_{n}(\theta)-\mathbb{M}_{n}(\theta_{0})\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+\alpha_{0}-\alpha\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi_{i})\right)\mathds{1}_{X_{i}\leq d_{0}\wedge d}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+\alpha_{0}-\beta\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi_{i})\right)\mathds{1}_{d<X_{i}\leq d_{0}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+\beta_{0}-\alpha\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi_{i})\right)\mathds{1}_{d_{0}<X_{i}\leq d}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+\beta_{0}-\beta\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi_{i})\right)\mathds{1}_{X_{i}>d\vee d_{0}}

and the corresponding population deterministic process:

\displaystyle\mathbb{M}(\theta)-\mathbb{M}(\theta_{0})

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+\alpha_{0}-\alpha\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi_{i})\right)\right]\mathbb{P}\left(X\leq d\wedge d_{0}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+\alpha_{0}-\beta\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi_{i})\right)\right]\mathbb{P}\left(d<X<d_{0}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+\beta_{0}-\alpha\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi_{i})\right)\right]\mathbb{P}\left(d_{0}<X<d\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+\beta_{0}-\beta\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi_{i})\right)\right]\mathbb{P}\left(X>d\vee d_{0}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{C_{k}}{2}\left[(\alpha_{0}-\alpha)^{2}\mathbb{P}\left(X\leq d\wedge d_{0}\right)+(\beta_{0}-\beta)^{2}\mathbb{P}\left(X>d\vee d_{0}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 100.00015pt\left.+|d-d_{0}|\left\{2(\alpha_{0}-\beta_{0}-\delta)^{2}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\geq C_{k}\left[(\alpha_{0}-\alpha)^{2}+(\beta_{0}-\beta)^{2}+|d-d_{0}|\right]

(A.14)

for all
|\alpha-\alpha_{0}|\leq\delta,|\beta-\beta_{0}|\leq\delta
, where the penultimate inequality follows from Lemma
A.8
. Also note that the definition of
C_{k}
is different in the last two lines, but as they are constant, we refrain ourselves from using different notations in each line.

Consistency:

We have established that
\mathbb{M}(\theta)
has local quadratic curvature with respect to
(\alpha,\beta)
in a
\delta-
neighbourhood around the truth. Now to establish the rate of convergence, we first need to establish the consistency of our estimator. To that end, we use Theorem 3.2.2 of
[
29
]
. That the process
\mathbb{M}(\theta)-\mathbb{M}(\theta_{0})
is continuous with respect to
\theta
and has a clear minima at
\theta=\theta_{0}
is immediate from the definition. Also the tightness of the minimizer
\hat{\theta}=(\hat{\alpha},\hat{\beta},\hat{d})
follows directly from our assumption of the compact parameter space
\Theta
. Therefore, all we need to show is that for any compact subset
K\subseteq\Theta
:

\sup_{\theta\in K}\left|\left(\mathbb{M}_{n}(\theta)-\mathbb{M}_{n}(\theta_{0})\right)-\left(\mathbb{M}(\theta)-\mathbb{M}(\theta_{0})\right)\right|=o_{p}(1)\,.

Towards that direction, define the function
f_{\theta}(X,\xi)
as:

\displaystyle f_{\theta}(X,\xi)

\displaystyle=\left(\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi+\alpha_{0}-\alpha\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi)\right)\mathds{1}_{X\leq d_{0}\wedge d}

\displaystyle\qquad\left(\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi+\alpha_{0}-\beta\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi)\right)\mathds{1}_{d<X\leq d_{0}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\left(\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi+\beta_{0}-\alpha\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi)\right)\mathds{1}_{d_{0}<X\leq d}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\left(\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi+\beta_{0}-\beta\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi)\right)\mathds{1}_{X>d\vee d_{0}}

It is immediate from the definition of
f_{\theta}(X,\xi)
that the collection of functions:

\mathcal{F}_{K}=\left\{f_{\theta}:\theta\in K\right\}

has finite VC dimension. Furthermore, as
K
is compact, there exist
c
such that:

\max_{\theta\in K}\left\{\left|\alpha\right|,\left|\beta\right|,\left|d\right|\right\}\leq c\,.

Note that the Huber function
H_{k}
is Lipschitz with the Lipschitz constant being
k
. Therefore we have for any
\mu>0
:

\displaystyle\left|\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi+\mu)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi)\right|

\displaystyle=\frac{k+1}{k}\left|H_{k}(\xi+\mu)-H_{k}(\xi)\right|\leq k|\mu|\,.

(A.15)

This implies that the function of
\mathcal{F}
are uniformly bounded. Hence using Glivenko-Cantelli theorem (e.g. see Theorem 2.8.1 of
[
29
]
) we conclude that:

\displaystyle\sup_{\theta\in K}\left|\left(\mathbb{M}_{n}(\theta)-\mathbb{M}_{n}(\theta_{0})\right)-\left(\mathbb{M}(\theta)-\mathbb{M}(\theta_{0})\right)\right|

\displaystyle=\left\|\left(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P\right)\right\|_{\mathcal{F}}=o_{p}(1)\,.

This establishes the consistency.

Tightness upon proper scaling:

We next show that:

\max\left\{\sqrt{n}\left(\hat{\alpha}-\alpha_{0}\right),\sqrt{n}\left(\hat{\beta}-\beta_{0}\right),n\left(\hat{d}-d_{0}\right)\right\}=O_{p}(1)\,.

Here we apply Theorem 3.2.5 of
[
29
]
. Define a semi-metric on
\Theta
as:

d\left(\theta_{1},\theta_{2}\right)=\sqrt{\left(\alpha_{1}-\alpha_{2}\right)^{2}+\left(\beta_{1}-\beta_{2}\right)^{2}+\left|d_{1}-d_{2}\right|}

From (
A.5
) we have
\mathbb{M}(\theta)-\mathbb{M}(\theta_{0})\geq C_{k}d^{2}(\theta,\theta_{0})
. To establish asymptotic equicontinuity of the process we need to bound:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{d(\theta,\theta_{0})\leq\delta}\left|\mathbb{M}_{n}(\theta)-\mathbb{M}_{n}(\theta_{0})-\left(\mathbb{M}(\theta)-\mathbb{M}(\theta_{0})\right)\right|\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{d(\theta,\theta_{0})\leq\delta}\left|\left(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P\right)f_{\theta}\right|\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\mathbb{P}_{n}-P\right\|_{\mathcal{F}_{\delta}}\right]

where we define the collection
\mathcal{F}_{\delta}
as
\mathcal{F}_{\delta}=\left\{f_{\theta}:d(\theta,\theta_{0})\leq\delta\right\}
. The envelope function of
\mathcal{F}_{\delta}
is defined as:

\displaystyle\sup_{\theta:d(\theta,\theta_{0})\leq\delta}\left|\left(\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi+\alpha_{0}-\alpha\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi)\right)\right|\mathds{1}_{X\leq d_{0}\wedge d}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\left|\left(\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi+\alpha_{0}-\beta\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi)\right)\right|\mathds{1}_{d<X\leq d_{0}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\left|\left(\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi+\beta_{0}-\alpha\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi)\right)\right|\mathds{1}_{d_{0}<X\leq d}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\left|\left(\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi+\beta_{0}-\beta\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi)\right)\right|\mathds{1}_{X>d\vee d_{0}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{k}\left(2\delta+\mathds{1}_{d<X\leq d_{0}}+\mathds{1}_{d_{0}<X\leq d}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2C_{k}\left(2\delta\vee\mathds{1}_{d<X\leq d_{0}}+\mathds{1}_{d_{0}<X\leq d}\right):=F_{\delta}(X,\xi)

Hence the
L_{2}(P)
norm of the envelope function:

\sqrt{PF_{\delta}^{2}}\leq 2C_{k}\left(2\delta\vee\sqrt{\mathbb{P}\left(d_{0}<X<d\right)+\mathbb{P}\left(d<X<d_{0}\right)}\right)\leq 4C_{k}\delta:=\phi_{n}(\delta)\,.

Hence an application of Theorem 3.2.5 of
[
29
]
yields
\sqrt{n}\ d\left(\hat{\theta},\theta_{0}\right)=O_{p}(1)
, which completes the proof.

Asymptotic distribution:

In the final paragraph we establish the asymptotic distribution of
\sqrt{n}\left(\hat{\alpha}-\alpha_{0}\right),\sqrt{n}\left(\hat{\beta}-\beta_{0}\right)
and
n\left(\hat{d}-d_{0}\right)
. Towards that end, we largely follow the approach of Subsection 14.5.1 of
[
17
]
. For any
\mathbf{h}:=(h_{1},h_{2},h_{3})\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
define a paramter vector
\theta_{n,\mathbf{h}}=\alpha_{0}+\frac{h_{1}}{\sqrt{n}},\beta_{0}+\frac{h_{2}}{\sqrt{n}},d_{0}+\frac{h_{3}}{n}
. Define a stochastic process
\mathbb{Q}_{n}
on
\mathbb{R}^{3}
as:

\displaystyle\mathbb{Q}_{n}(h_{1},h_{2},h_{3})

\displaystyle=n\times\mathbb{P}_{n}\left(f_{\theta_{n,\mathbf{h}}}-f_{\theta_{0}}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+\frac{h_{1}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi_{i})\right)\mathds{1}_{X_{i}\leq d_{0}\wedge d}

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+\alpha_{0}-\beta_{0}-\frac{h_{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi_{i})\right)\mathds{1}_{d_{0}+\frac{h_{3}}{n}<X_{i}\leq d_{0}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+\beta_{0}-\alpha_{0}-\frac{h_{1}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi_{i})\right)\mathds{1}_{d_{0}<X_{i}\leq d_{0}+\frac{h_{3}}{n}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+\frac{h_{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi_{i})\right)\mathds{1}_{X_{i}>d\vee d_{0}}

\displaystyle:=\mathbb{Q}_{n,1}(\mathbf{h})+\mathbb{Q}_{n,2}(\mathbf{h})+\mathbb{Q}_{n,3}(\mathbf{h})+\mathbb{Q}_{n,4}(\mathbf{h})\,.

It is immediate from the definition of
\mathbb{Q}_{n}(\mathbf{h})
that:

\widehat{\mathbf{h}}_{n}:=\left(\sqrt{n}\left(\hat{\alpha}-\alpha_{0}\right),\sqrt{n}\left(\hat{\beta}-\beta_{0}\right),n\left(\hat{d}-d_{0}\right)\right)=\text{mid argmin}_{\mathbf{h}\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbb{Q}_{n}(\mathbf{h})\,.

We next show that there exist a stochastic process
\mathbb{Q}
on
\mathbb{R}^{3}
such that for any compact rectangle
\mathbb{I}=I_{1}\times I_{2}\times I_{3}\subset\mathbb{R}^{3}
:

\mathbb{Q}_{n}|_{\mathbb{I}}\overset{\mathscr{L}}{\implies}\mathbb{Q}|_{\mathbb{I}}\,.

where the process
\mathbb{Q}
is defined as:

\displaystyle\mathbb{Q}(\mathbf{h})

\displaystyle=\left(h_{1}\sigma_{k}\sqrt{F_{X}(d_{0})}\times Z_{1}+\frac{h_{1}^{2}}{2}\mu_{k}F_{X}(d_{0})\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 50.00008pt+\left(h_{2}\sigma_{k}\sqrt{\bar{F}_{X}(d_{0})}\times Z_{2}+\frac{h_{2}^{2}}{2}\mu_{k}\bar{F}_{X}(d_{0})\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 100.00015pt+\text{CPP}\left(\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+(\alpha_{0}-\beta_{0})\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi_{i}),f_{X}(\theta_{0})\right)\,.

with
Z_{1},Z_{2}\overset{i.i.d}{\sim}\mathcal{N}(0,1)
and CPP is (as defined in the main paper) compound Poisson process. Note that the stochastic process
\mathbb{Q}_{n}
is continuous with respect to its first two co-ordinates and cadlag (right continuous with left limit) with respect to its third co-ordinate. Hence to establish the convergence of
\left\{\mathbb{Q}_{n}|_{\mathbb{I}}\right\}_{n\in\mathbb{N}}
we need to use Skorohod topology. We mainly use Theorem 13.5 of
[
7
]
to establish the convergence result. Towards that end, define:

\displaystyle\tilde{\xi}_{i,h_{1}}

\displaystyle=\left(\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+\frac{h_{1}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi_{i})\right)-\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+\frac{h_{1}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi_{i})\right)\right]

\displaystyle\tilde{\xi}_{i,h_{2}}

\displaystyle=\left(\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+\frac{h_{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi_{i})\right)-\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+\frac{h_{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi_{i})\right)\right]

and another stochastic process
\tilde{\mathbb{Q}}_{n}(\mathbf{h})
as:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathbb{Q}}_{n}(\mathbf{h})

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\tilde{\xi}_{i,h_{1}}\mathds{1}_{X_{i}\leq d_{0}\wedge d_{0}+\frac{h_{3}}{n}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+(\alpha_{0}-\beta_{0})\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi_{i})\right)\mathds{1}_{d_{0}+\frac{h_{3}}{n}<X_{i}\leq d_{0}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+(\beta_{0}-\alpha_{0})\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi_{i})\right)\mathds{1}_{d_{0}<X_{i}\leq d_{0}+\frac{h_{3}}{n}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\tilde{\xi}_{i,h_{2}}\mathds{1}_{X_{i}>d_{0}\vee d_{0}+\frac{h_{3}}{n}}

\displaystyle:=\tilde{\mathbb{Q}}_{n,+}(\mathbf{h})\mathds{1}_{h_{3}\geq 0}+\tilde{\mathbb{Q}}_{n,-}(\mathbf{h})\mathds{1}_{h_{3}<0}\,.

Hence we have the following decomposition:

\mathbb{Q}_{n}(\mathbf{h})=\tilde{\mathbb{Q}}_{n}(\mathbf{h})+\mathfrak{E}_{n}(\mathbf{h})+\mathfrak{R}_{n}(\mathbf{h})

(A.16)

where:

\displaystyle\mathfrak{R}_{n}(\mathbf{h})

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\tilde{H}_{k}\left({\epsilon}_{i}+(\alpha_{0}-\beta_{0})-\frac{h_{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}\left({\epsilon}_{i}+(\alpha_{0}-\beta_{0})\right)\right)\mathds{1}_{d_{0}+\frac{h_{3}}{n}<X_{i}\leq d_{0}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\tilde{H}_{k}\left({\epsilon}_{i}+(\beta_{0}-\alpha_{0})-\frac{h_{1}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}\left({\epsilon}_{i}+(\beta_{0}-\alpha_{0})\right)\right)\mathds{1}_{d_{0}<X_{i}\leq d_{0}+\frac{h_{3}}{n}}

and

\displaystyle\mathfrak{E}_{n}(\mathbf{h})

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+\frac{h_{1}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}\right)\right)\right]\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathds{1}_{X_{i}\leq d_{0}\wedge d_{0}+\frac{h_{3}}{n}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+\frac{h_{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}\right)\right)\right]\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathds{1}_{X_{i}>d_{0}\vee d_{0}+\frac{h_{3}}{n}}

We next show
\mathfrak{R}_{n}(\mathbf{h})
is
o_{p}(1)
uniformly over a compact set:

\displaystyle\left|\mathfrak{R}_{n}(\mathbf{h})\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+(\alpha_{0}-\beta_{0})-\frac{h_{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+(\alpha_{0}-\beta_{0})\right)\right)\mathds{1}_{d_{0}+\frac{h_{3}}{n}<X_{i}\leq d_{0}}\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\left.\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+(\beta_{0}-\alpha_{0})-\frac{h_{1}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+(\beta_{0}-\alpha_{0})\right)\right)\mathds{1}_{d_{0}<X_{i}\leq d_{0}+\frac{h_{3}}{n}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left|\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+(\alpha_{0}-\beta_{0})-\frac{h_{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+(\alpha_{0}-\beta_{0})\right)\right|\mathds{1}_{d_{0}+\frac{h_{3}}{n}<X_{i}\leq d_{0}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left|\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+(\beta_{0}-\alpha_{0})-\frac{h_{1}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+(\beta_{0}-\alpha_{0})\right)\right|\mathds{1}_{d_{0}<X_{i}\leq d_{0}+\frac{h_{3}}{n}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(k+1)h_{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathds{1}_{d_{0}+\frac{h_{3}}{n}<X_{i}\leq d_{0}}+\frac{(k+1)h_{1}}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathds{1}_{d_{0}<X_{i}\leq d_{0}+\frac{h_{3}}{n}}

Now suppose
\mathbf{h}\in\mathbb{I}
. There
|h_{1}|\vee|h_{2}|\vee|h_{3}|\leq K
for some
K>0
. Hence we have:

\displaystyle\sup_{\mathbf{h}\in\mathbb{I}}\left|\mathfrak{R}_{n}(\mathbf{h})\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(k+1)K}{\sqrt{n}}\left[\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathds{1}_{d_{0}-\frac{K}{n}<X_{i}\leq d_{0}}+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathds{1}_{d_{0}<X_{i}\leq d_{0}+\frac{K}{n}}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{(k+1)K}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathds{1}_{d_{0}-\frac{K}{n}<X_{i}\leq d_{0}+\frac{K}{n}}

as we know:

\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathds{1}_{d_{0}-\frac{K}{n}<X_{i}\leq d_{0}+\frac{K}{n}}\overset{\mathscr{L}}{\implies}\text{Pois}\left(Kf_{X}(d_{0})\right)

we conclude that:

\sup_{\mathbf{h}\in\mathbb{I}}\left|\mathfrak{R}_{n}(\mathbf{h})\right|=O_{p}(n^{-1/2})=o_{p}(1)\,.

(A.17)

We now establish the convergence of
\mathfrak{E}(\mathbf{h})
:

\displaystyle\mathfrak{E}_{n}(\mathbf{h})

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+\frac{h_{1}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi_{i})\right)\right]\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathds{1}_{X_{i}\leq d_{0}\wedge d_{0}+\frac{h_{3}}{n}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+\frac{h_{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi_{i})\right)\right]\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathds{1}_{X_{i}>d_{0}\vee d_{0}+\frac{h_{3}}{n}}

\displaystyle=\frac{k+1}{k}\left\{\frac{h_{1}^{2}}{2n}\mathbb{P}\left(-k\leq\xi\leq k-\frac{h_{1}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)+\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{h_{1}}{\sqrt{n}}k-\frac{h_{1}}{\sqrt{n}}\xi-\frac{1}{2}\left(\xi-k\right)^{2}\right)\mathds{1}_{k-\frac{h_{1}}{\sqrt{n}}\leq\xi\leq k}\right]\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\left.\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{2}\left(\xi+\frac{h_{1}}{\sqrt{n}}+k\right)^{2}\mathds{1}_{-k-\frac{h_{1}}{\sqrt{n}}\leq\xi\leq-k}\right]\right\}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathds{1}_{X_{i}\leq d_{0}\wedge d_{0}+\frac{h_{3}}{n}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{k+1}{k}\left\{\frac{h_{2}^{2}}{2n}\mathbb{P}\left(-k\leq\xi\leq k-\frac{h_{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)+\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{h_{2}}{\sqrt{n}}k-\frac{h_{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\xi-\frac{1}{2}\left(\xi-k\right)^{2}\right)\mathds{1}_{k-\frac{h_{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\leq\xi\leq k}\right]\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\left.\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{2}\left(\xi+\frac{h_{2}}{\sqrt{n}}+k\right)^{2}\mathds{1}_{-k-\frac{h_{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\leq\xi\leq-k}\right]\right\}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathds{1}_{X_{i}>d_{0}\vee d_{0}+\frac{h_{3}}{n}}

\displaystyle=n\frac{k+1}{k}\left\{\frac{h_{1}^{2}}{2n}\mathbb{P}\left(-k\leq\xi\leq k-\frac{h_{1}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)+\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{h_{1}}{\sqrt{n}}k-\frac{h_{1}}{\sqrt{n}}\xi-\frac{1}{2}\left(\xi-k\right)^{2}\right)\mathds{1}_{k-\frac{h_{1}}{\sqrt{n}}\leq\xi\leq k}\right]\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\left.\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{2}\left(\xi+\frac{h_{1}}{\sqrt{n}}+k\right)^{2}\mathds{1}_{-k-\frac{h_{1}}{\sqrt{n}}\leq\xi\leq-k}\right]\right\}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathds{1}_{X_{i}\leq d_{0}\wedge d_{0}+\frac{h_{3}}{n}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+n\frac{k+1}{k}\left\{\frac{h_{2}^{2}}{2n}\mathbb{P}\left(-k\leq\xi\leq k-\frac{h_{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)+\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{h_{2}}{\sqrt{n}}k-\frac{h_{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\xi-\frac{1}{2}\left(\xi-k\right)^{2}\right)\mathds{1}_{k-\frac{h_{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\leq\xi\leq k}\right]\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\left.\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{2}\left(\xi+\frac{h_{2}}{\sqrt{n}}+k\right)^{2}\mathds{1}_{-k-\frac{h_{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\leq\xi\leq-k}\right]\right\}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathds{1}_{X_{i}>d_{0}\vee d_{0}+\frac{h_{3}}{n}}

\displaystyle=\frac{k+1}{k}\left\{\frac{h_{1}^{2}}{2}\mathbb{P}\left(-k\leq\xi\leq k-\frac{h_{1}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)+n\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{h_{1}}{\sqrt{n}}k-\frac{h_{1}}{\sqrt{n}}\xi-\frac{1}{2}\left(\xi-k\right)^{2}\right)\mathds{1}_{k-\frac{h_{1}}{\sqrt{n}}\leq\xi\leq k}\right]\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\left.n\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{2}\left(\xi+\frac{h_{1}}{\sqrt{n}}+k\right)^{2}\mathds{1}_{-k-\frac{h_{1}}{\sqrt{n}}\leq\xi\leq-k}\right]\right\}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathds{1}_{X_{i}\leq d_{0}\wedge d_{0}+\frac{h_{3}}{n}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{k+1}{k}\left\{\frac{h_{2}^{2}}{2}\mathbb{P}\left(-k\leq\xi\leq k-\frac{h_{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)+n\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{h_{2}}{\sqrt{n}}k-\frac{h_{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\xi-\frac{1}{2}\left(\xi-k\right)^{2}\right)\mathds{1}_{k-\frac{h_{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\leq\xi\leq k}\right]\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\left.n\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{2}\left(\xi+\frac{h_{2}}{\sqrt{n}}+k\right)^{2}\mathds{1}_{-k-\frac{h_{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\leq\xi\leq-k}\right]\right\}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathds{1}_{X_{i}>d_{0}\vee d_{0}+\frac{h_{3}}{n}}

From strong law of large numbers we have:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathds{1}_{X_{i}\leq d_{0}\wedge d_{0}+\frac{h_{3}}{n}}

\displaystyle\overset{p}{\longrightarrow}\mathbb{P}(X\leq d_{0})\,,

(A.18)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathds{1}_{X_{i}>d_{0}\vee d_{0}+\frac{h_{3}}{n}}

\displaystyle\overset{p}{\longrightarrow}\mathbb{P}(X>d_{0})\,,

(A.19)

For the other terms in the expectation:

\displaystyle\frac{h_{1}^{2}}{2}\mathbb{P}\left(-k\leq\xi\leq k-\frac{h_{1}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{}{\longrightarrow}\frac{h_{1}^{2}}{2}\mathbb{P}\left(-k\leq{\epsilon}\leq k\right)\,,

(A.20)

\displaystyle\frac{h_{2}^{2}}{2}\mathbb{P}\left(-k\leq\xi\leq k-\frac{h_{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{}{\longrightarrow}\frac{h_{2}^{2}}{2}\mathbb{P}\left(-k\leq{\epsilon}\leq k\right)\,.

(A.21)

For the other terms:

\displaystyle n\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{h_{2}}{\sqrt{n}}k-\frac{h_{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\xi-\frac{1}{2}\left(\xi-k\right)^{2}\right)\mathds{1}_{k-\frac{h_{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\leq\xi\leq k}\right]

\displaystyle=n\int_{k-\frac{h_{2}}{\sqrt{n}}}^{k}\left(\frac{h_{2}}{\sqrt{n}}k-\frac{h_{2}}{\sqrt{n}}x-\frac{1}{2}\left(x-k\right)^{2}\right)f_{\xi}(x)\ dx

\displaystyle=n\left[\frac{h_{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\int_{k-\frac{h_{2}}{\sqrt{n}}}^{k}(k-x)f_{\xi}(x)\ dx-\frac{1}{2}\int_{k-\frac{h_{2}}{\sqrt{n}}}^{k}\left(x-k\right)^{2}\ f_{\xi}(x)\ dx\right]

\displaystyle=n\left[\frac{h_{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\int_{0}^{\frac{h_{2}}{\sqrt{n}}}zf_{\xi}(z-k)\ dx-\frac{1}{2}\int_{-\frac{h_{2}}{\sqrt{n}}}^{0}z^{2}\ f_{\xi}(z+k)\ dx\right]

\displaystyle\leq Cn\left[\frac{h_{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\int_{0}^{\frac{h_{2}}{\sqrt{n}}}z\ dz+\frac{1}{2}\int_{-\frac{h_{2}}{\sqrt{n}}}^{0}z^{2}\ dz\right]\hskip 14.45377pt[C=\max_{x}f_{\xi}(x)]

\displaystyle=n\times O(n^{-3/2})=o(1)\,.

(A.22)

and

\displaystyle n\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{2}\left(\xi+\frac{h_{2}}{\sqrt{n}}+k\right)^{2}\mathds{1}_{-k-\frac{h_{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\leq\xi\leq-k}\right]

\displaystyle=n\int_{-k-\frac{h_{2}}{\sqrt{n}}}^{-k}\frac{1}{2}\left(x+\frac{h_{2}}{\sqrt{n}}+k\right)^{2}\ f_{\xi}(x)\ dx

\displaystyle=\frac{h_{2}^{3}}{6n^{1/2}}f_{\xi}(k)+o(1)=o(1)\,.

(A.23)

Similar calculation holds for the terms involving
h_{1}
. Hence we conclude combining equations (
A.18
) - (
A.23
) we conclude:

\displaystyle\mathfrak{E}_{n}(\mathbf{h})

\displaystyle\overset{P}{\longrightarrow}\frac{k+1}{k}\left[\frac{h_{1}^{2}}{2}\mathbb{P}\left(-k\leq{\epsilon}\leq k\right)F_{X}(d_{0})+\frac{h_{2}^{2}}{2}\mathbb{P}\left(-k\leq{\epsilon}\leq k\right)\bar{F}_{X}(d_{0})\right]\,.

(A.24)

Finally we show the weak convergence of
\tilde{\mathbb{Q}}_{n}(\mathbf{h})
to
\mathbb{Q}(\mathbf{h})
, for which we need to show that the collection
\{\tilde{\mathbb{Q}}_{n}(\mathbf{h})\}_{n\in\mathbb{N}}
is tight with respect to appropriate topology and every finite dimensional projection of
\tilde{\mathbb{Q}}_{n}(\mathbf{h})
converges to that of
\mathbb{Q}(\mathbf{h})
. We embark on by showing that for any fixed
\mathbf{h}
,
\tilde{\mathbb{Q}}_{n}(\mathbf{h})
converges to
\mathbb{Q}(\mathbf{h})
in distribution.
Towards that direction, fix
h_{3}>0
:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{\sf exp}{\left(it\tilde{\mathbb{Q}}_{n}(\mathbf{h})\right)}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{E}\left[{\sf exp}{\left(it\tilde{\mathbb{Q}}_{n}(\mathbf{h})\right)}\mid X_{1},\dots,X_{n}\right]\right]

\displaystyle:=\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{X}}\left[{\sf exp}{\left(it\tilde{\mathbb{Q}}_{n}(\mathbf{h})\right)}\right]\right]

We start with analyzing the inner expectation
\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{X}}\left[{\sf exp}{\left(it\tilde{\mathbb{Q}}_{n}(\mathbf{h})\right)}\right]
:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{X}}\left[{\sf exp}{\left(it\tilde{\mathbb{Q}}_{n}(\mathbf{h})\right)}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{X}}\left[{\sf exp}\left\{\left(it\sum_{i=1}^{n}\tilde{\xi}_{i,h_{1}}\mathds{1}_{X_{i}\leq d_{0}}\right.\right.\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\left.\left.\left.+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+(\alpha_{0}-\beta_{0})\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi_{i})\right)\mathds{1}_{d_{0}<X_{i}\leq d_{0}+\frac{h_{3}}{n}}\right.\right.\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 200.0003pt\left.\left.\left.+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\tilde{\xi}_{i,h_{2}}\mathds{1}_{X_{i}>d_{0}+\frac{h_{3}}{n}}\right)\right\}\right]

\displaystyle=\left(\phi_{\tilde{\xi}_{h_{1}}}(t)\right)^{\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathds{1}_{X_{i}\leq d_{0}}}\times\left(\phi_{\tilde{\xi}_{h_{2}}}(t)\right)^{\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathds{1}_{X_{i}>d_{0}+\frac{h_{3}}{n}}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\left(\phi_{\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+(\alpha_{0}-\beta_{0})\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi_{i})}(t)\right)^{\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathds{1}_{d_{0}<X_{i}\leq d_{0}+\frac{h_{3}}{n}}}

\displaystyle=\left(\phi_{\tilde{\xi}_{h_{1}}}(t)\right)^{n\times\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathds{1}_{X_{i}\leq d_{0}}}\times\left(\phi_{\tilde{\xi}_{h_{2}}}(t)\right)^{n\times\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathds{1}_{X_{i}>d_{0}+\frac{h_{3}}{n}}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\left(\phi_{\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+(\alpha_{0}-\beta_{0})\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi_{i})}(t)\right)^{\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathds{1}_{d_{0}<X_{i}\leq d_{0}+\frac{h_{3}}{n}}}

(A.25)

To show the convergence of the characteristic function of
\tilde{\xi}_{h_{1}}
(and similarly for
\tilde{\xi}_{h_{2}}
) we first note that the variance of
\tilde{\xi}_{h_{1}}
for
h_{1}>0
is:

\displaystyle{\sf var}\left(\tilde{\xi}\right)

\displaystyle={\sf var}\left(\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+\frac{h_{1}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi_{i})\right)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+\frac{h_{1}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi_{i})\right)^{2}\right]+O(n^{-2})\hskip 14.45377pt[O(n^{-2})\text{ follows from the analysis of }\mathfrak{E}_{n}(\mathbf{h})]

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{k+1}{k}\right)^{2}\left\{\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{h_{1}}{\sqrt{n}}\xi+\frac{h_{1}^{2}}{2n}\right)^{2}\mathds{1}_{-k\leq\xi\leq k-\frac{h_{1}}{\sqrt{n}}}\right]\right.

\displaystyle\qquad+\left.\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{1}{2}\left(\xi+\frac{h_{1}}{\sqrt{n}}+k\right)^{2}-\frac{h_{1}}{\sqrt{n}}k\right)^{2}\mathds{1}_{-k-\frac{h_{1}}{\sqrt{n}}\leq\xi\leq-k}\right]\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\left.\mathbb{E}\left[\left(-\frac{1}{2}\left(\xi-k\right)^{2}+k\frac{h_{1}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)^{2}\mathds{1}_{k-\frac{h_{1}}{\sqrt{n}}\leq\xi\leq k}\right]\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\left.\frac{k^{2}h_{1}^{2}}{n}\left\{\mathbb{P}\left(\xi>k\right)+\mathbb{P}\left(\xi<-k-\frac{h_{1}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)\right\}+O(n^{-2})\right\}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{k+1}{k}\right)^{2}\left(\frac{h_{1}^{2}}{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\xi^{2}\mathds{1}_{-k\leq\xi\leq k}\right]+\frac{k^{2}h_{1}^{2}}{n}\left\{\mathbb{P}\left(\xi>k\right)+\mathbb{P}\left(\xi<-k\right)\right\}\right)+o(n^{-1})

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{k+1}{k}\right)^{2}\left(\frac{h_{1}^{2}}{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\xi^{2}\mathds{1}_{-k\leq\xi\leq k}\right]+\frac{2k^{2}h_{1}^{2}}{n}\mathbb{P}\left(\xi>k\right)\right)+o(n^{-1})

\displaystyle:=\frac{h_{1}^{2}\sigma_{k}^{2}}{n}+o(n^{-1})\,.

where the variance parameter
\sigma_{k}^{2}
is defined as:

\left(\frac{k+1}{k}\right)^{2}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\xi^{2}\mathds{1}_{-k\leq\xi\leq k}\right]+2k^{2}\mathbb{P}\left(\xi>k\right)\right)\,.

Similar calculation holds for
h_{1}<0
and for
h_{2}
. Hence going back to equation (
A.5
) we have:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{X}}\left[{\sf exp}{\left(it\tilde{\mathbb{Q}}_{n}(\mathbf{h})\right)}\right]

\displaystyle=\left(1-\frac{t^{2}}{2}\frac{h_{1}^{2}\sigma_{k}^{2}}{n}+o(n^{-1})\right)^{n\times\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathds{1}_{X_{i}\leq d_{0}}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\left(\phi_{\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+(\alpha_{0}-\beta_{0})\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi_{i})}(t)\right)^{\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathds{1}_{d_{0}<X_{i}\leq d_{0}+\frac{h_{3}}{n}}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\left(1-\frac{t^{2}}{2}\frac{h_{2}^{2}\sigma_{k}^{2}}{n}+o(n^{-1})\right)^{n\times\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathds{1}_{X_{i}>d_{0}+\frac{h_{3}}{n}}}

As

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathds{1}_{X_{i}\leq d_{0}}

\displaystyle\overset{a.s.}{\longrightarrow}F_{X}(d_{0})

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathds{1}_{X_{i}>d_{0}+\frac{h_{3}}{n}}

\displaystyle\overset{a.s.}{\longrightarrow}\bar{F}_{X}(d_{0})

we conclude:

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{t^{2}}{2}\frac{h_{1}^{2}\sigma_{k}^{2}}{n}+o(n^{-1})\right)^{n\times\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathds{1}_{X_{i}\leq d_{0}}}

\displaystyle\overset{a.s.}{\longrightarrow}e^{-\frac{t^{2}h_{1}^{2}\sigma_{k}^{2}}{2}F_{X}(d_{0})}

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{t^{2}}{2}\frac{h_{2}^{2}\sigma_{k}^{2}}{n}+o(n^{-1})\right)^{n\times\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathds{1}_{X_{i}>d_{0}+\frac{h_{3}}{n}}}

\displaystyle\overset{a.s.}{\longrightarrow}e^{-\frac{t^{2}h_{2}^{2}\sigma_{k}^{2}}{2}\bar{F}_{X}(d_{0})}

Also we know:

\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathds{1}_{d_{0}<X_{i}\leq d_{0}+\frac{h_{3}}{n}}\overset{\mathscr{L}}{\implies}\text{Pois}\left(f_{X}(d_{0})h_{3}\right)

which further implies:

\left(\phi_{\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+(\alpha_{0}-\beta_{0})\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi_{i})}(t)\right)^{\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathds{1}_{d_{0}<X_{i}\leq d_{0}+\frac{h_{3}}{n}}}\overset{\mathscr{L}}{\implies}\left(\phi_{\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+(\alpha_{0}-\beta_{0})\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi_{i})}(t)\right)^{\text{Pois}\left(f_{X}(\theta_{0})h_{3}\right)}

Hence combining these we conclude:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{X}}\left[{\sf exp}{\left(it\tilde{\mathbb{Q}}_{n}(\mathbf{h})\right)}\right]

\displaystyle=\left(\phi_{\tilde{\xi}_{h_{1}}}(t)\right)^{n\times\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathds{1}_{X_{i}\leq d_{0}}}\times\left(\phi_{\tilde{\xi}_{h_{2}}}(t)\right)^{n\times\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathds{1}_{X_{i}>d_{0}+\frac{h_{3}}{n}}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\left(\phi_{\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+(\alpha_{0}-\beta_{0})\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi_{i})}(t)\right)^{\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathds{1}_{d_{0}<X_{i}\leq d_{0}+\frac{h_{3}}{n}}}

\displaystyle\overset{\mathscr{L}}{\implies}e^{-\frac{t^{2}h_{1}^{2}\sigma_{k}^{2}}{2}F_{X}(d_{0})}\times e^{-\frac{t^{2}h_{2}^{2}\sigma_{k}^{2}}{2}\bar{F}_{X}(d_{0})}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\left(\phi_{\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+(\alpha_{0}-\beta_{0})\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi_{i})}(t)\right)^{\text{Pois}\left(f_{X}(d_{0})h_{3}\right)}

Applying DCT and taking expectation on the both side we conclude:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{\sf exp}{\left(it\tilde{\mathbb{Q}}_{n}(\mathbf{h})\right)}\right]

\displaystyle\longrightarrow e^{-\frac{t^{2}h_{1}^{2}\sigma_{k}^{2}}{2}F_{X}(d_{0})}\times e^{-\frac{t^{2}h_{2}^{2}\sigma_{k}^{2}}{2}\bar{F}_{X}(d_{0})}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\phi_{\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+(\alpha_{0}-\beta_{0})\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi_{i})}(t)\right)^{\text{Pois}\left(f_{X}(d_{0})h_{3}\right)}\right]\,.

This concludes that
\tilde{\mathbb{Q}}_{n}(\mathbf{h})\overset{\mathscr{L}}{\implies}\mathbb{Q}(\mathbf{h})
. The proof of the fact that for any finite collection
(\mathbf{h}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{h}_{l})
:

\left(\tilde{\mathbb{Q}}_{n}(\mathbf{h}_{1}),\dots,\tilde{\mathbb{Q}}_{n}(\mathbf{h}_{l})\right)\overset{\mathscr{L}}{\implies}\left(\mathbb{Q}(\mathbf{h}_{1}),\dots,\mathbb{Q}(\mathbf{h}_{l})\right)

(A.26)

is similar (same analysis of characteristic function) and hence skipped for brevity. Interested readers can take a look at the proof of Lemma 3.2 of
[
20
]
or the proof of Theorem 5 of
[
18
]
for more details of this type of calculations. We next establish the tightness of the process. Define another process
\accentset{\approx}{\mathbb{Q}}_{n}(\mathbf{h})
as:

\displaystyle\accentset{\approx}{\mathbb{Q}}_{n}(\mathbf{h})

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\tilde{\xi}_{i,h_{1}}\mathds{1}_{X_{i}\leq d_{0}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(H_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+(\alpha_{0}-\beta_{0})\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi_{i})\right)\mathds{1}_{d_{0}+\frac{h_{3}}{n}<X_{i}\leq d_{0}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(H_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+(\alpha_{0}-\beta_{0})\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi_{i})\right)\mathds{1}_{d_{0}<X_{i}\leq d_{0}+\frac{h_{3}}{n}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\tilde{\xi}_{i,h_{2}}\mathds{1}_{X_{i}>d_{0}}

\displaystyle:=\accentset{\approx}{\mathbb{Q}}_{n,1}(\mathbf{h})+\accentset{\approx}{\mathbb{Q}}_{n,2}(\mathbf{h})+\accentset{\approx}{\mathbb{Q}}_{n,3}(\mathbf{h})+\accentset{\approx}{\mathbb{Q}}_{n,4}(\mathbf{h})

(A.27)

We now show that
\accentset{\approx}{\mathbb{Q}}_{n}(\mathbf{h})
uniformly approximate
\tilde{\mathbb{Q}}_{n}(\mathbf{h})
over compact sets:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{\mathbf{h}\in\mathbb{I}}\left|\accentset{\approx}{\mathbb{Q}}_{n}(\mathbf{h})-\tilde{\mathbb{Q}}_{n}(\mathbf{h})\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{\mathbf{h}\in\mathbb{I}}\left\{\left|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\tilde{\xi}_{i,h_{1}}\left[\mathds{1}_{X_{i}\leq d_{0}}-\mathds{1}_{X_{i}\leq d_{0}\wedge d_{0}+\frac{h_{3}}{n}}\right]\right|+\left|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\tilde{\xi}_{i,h_{2}}\left[\mathds{1}_{X_{i}>d_{0}}-\mathds{1}_{X_{i}>d_{0}\vee d_{0}+\frac{h_{3}}{n}}\right]\right|\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{\mathbf{h}\in\mathbb{I}}\left|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\tilde{\xi}_{i,h_{1}}\left[\mathds{1}_{X_{i}\leq d_{0}}-\mathds{1}_{X_{i}\leq d_{0}\wedge d_{0}+\frac{h_{3}}{n}}\right]\right|\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 100.00015pt+2\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{\mathbf{h}\in\mathbb{I}}\left|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\tilde{\xi}_{i,h_{2}}\left[\mathds{1}_{X_{i}\leq d_{0}}-\mathds{1}_{X_{i}>d_{0}\vee d_{0}+\frac{h_{3}}{n}}\right]\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{i=1}^{n}|\tilde{\xi}_{i,h_{1}}|\mathds{1}_{d_{0}-\frac{K}{n}<X_{i}\leq d_{0}}\right]+2\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{i=1}^{n}|\tilde{\xi}_{i,h_{2}}|\mathds{1}_{d_{0}<X_{i}\leq d_{0}+\frac{K}{n}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2n\mathbb{E}\left[|\tilde{\xi}_{h_{1}}|\right]\mathbb{P}\left(d_{0}-\frac{K}{n}<X\leq d_{0}\right)+2n\mathbb{E}\left[|\tilde{\xi}_{h_{2}}|\right]\mathbb{P}\left(d_{0}<X\leq d_{0}+\frac{K}{n}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2n\times\left[\sqrt{{\sf var}\left(\tilde{\xi}_{h_{1}}\right)}\times\left(\frac{K}{n}f_{X}(d_{0})+o(n^{-1})\right)+\sqrt{{\sf var}\left(\tilde{\xi}_{h_{2}}\right)}\times\left(\frac{K}{n}f_{X}(d_{0})+o(n^{-1})\right)\right]

\displaystyle=2n\times\left[\left(\frac{h_{1}\sigma_{k}}{\sqrt{n}}+o(n^{-1/2})\right)\times\left(\frac{K}{n}f_{X}(d_{0})+o(n^{-1})\right)\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\left.+\left(\frac{h_{2}\sigma_{k}}{\sqrt{n}}+o(n^{-1/2})\right)\times\left(\frac{K}{n}f_{X}(d_{0})+o(n^{-1})\right)\right]

\displaystyle=O(n^{-1/2})=o(1)\,.

(A.28)

Hence from equation (
A.28
) we conclude:

\sup_{\mathbf{h}\in\mathbb{I}}\left|\accentset{\approx}{\mathbb{Q}}_{n}(\mathbf{h})-\tilde{\mathbb{Q}}_{n}(\mathbf{h})\right|=o_{p}(1)\,.

(A.29)

Therefore it is immediate from equation (
A.26
) that:

\left(\accentset{\approx}{\mathbb{Q}}_{n}(\mathbf{h}_{1}),\dots,\accentset{\approx}{\mathbb{Q}}_{n}(\mathbf{h}_{l})\right)\overset{\mathscr{L}}{\implies}\left(\mathbb{Q}(\mathbf{h}_{1}),\dots,\mathbb{Q}(\mathbf{h}_{l})\right)\,.

(A.30)

Next, we show that tightness
\left\{\accentset{\approx}{\mathbb{Q}}_{n}(\mathbf{h})\right\}_{n\in\mathbb{N}}\,.
As evident from equation (
A.27
), it is enough to show tightness of
\left\{\accentset{\approx}{\mathbb{Q}}_{n,i}(\mathbf{h})\right\}_{n\in\mathbb{N}}
for
i=1,2,3,4
. For
i=1
, the process
\accentset{\approx}{\mathbb{Q}}_{n,1}(\mathbf{h})
only depends on
h_{1}
and hence have continuous paths. Therefore to establish tightness, it is enough to show:

\lim_{\delta\downarrow 0}\limsup_{n\to\infty}\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}|h_{1,1}|\vee|h_{1,2}|\leq K\\
|h_{1,1}-h_{1,2}|\leq\delta\end{subarray}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left|\tilde{\xi}_{i,h_{1,1}}-\tilde{\xi}_{i,h_{1,2}}\right|\mathds{1}_{X_{i}\leq d_{0}}\right]=0

Towards that end, define a collection of functions:

\mathcal{F}_{1,\delta}=\left\{f_{h_{1,1},h_{1,2}}:\left|h_{1,1}\right|\vee\left|h_{1,2}\right|\leq K,|h_{1,1}-h_{1,2}|\leq\delta\right\}\,,

where the individual functions
f_{h_{1,1},h_{1,2}}
is defined as:

\displaystyle f_{h_{1,1},h_{1,2}}(X,{\epsilon})

\displaystyle=\left\{\left(\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+\frac{h_{1}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+\frac{h_{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)\right)\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\left.-\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+\frac{h_{1}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+\frac{h_{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)\right)\right]\right\}\mathds{1}_{X\leq d_{0}}\,.

Clearly
\mathcal{F}_{1,\delta}
has finite VC dimension. Also from equation (
A.15
) we have:

\displaystyle\left|\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+\frac{h_{1,1}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+\frac{h_{1,2}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\frac{k+1}{k}\left[4k\frac{\left|h_{1,1}-h_{1,2}\right|}{\sqrt{n}}+\frac{1}{2}\frac{\left(h_{1,1}-h_{1,2}\right)^{2}}{n}+\left(\frac{|h_{1,1}-h_{1,2}|}{\sqrt{n}}\wedge 2k\right)^{2}\right]\leq C_{k}\frac{\delta}{\sqrt{n}}\,.

Therefore, the envelope function can be taken as:

F_{1,\delta}(X,{\epsilon})=\frac{2C_{k}\delta}{\sqrt{n}}\,.

Hence using Lemma 2.14.1 of
[
29
]
we conclude:

\limsup_{n\to\infty}\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}|h_{1,1}|\vee|h_{1,2}|\leq K\\
|h_{1,1}-h_{1,2}|\leq\delta\end{subarray}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left|\tilde{\xi}_{i,h_{1,1}}-\tilde{\xi}_{i,h_{1,2}}\right|\mathds{1}_{X_{i}\leq d_{0}}\right]\lesssim\delta

which established tightness of
\accentset{\approx}{\mathbb{Q}}_{n,1}(\mathbf{h})
. The proof of tightness of
\accentset{\approx}{\mathbb{Q}}_{n,4}(\mathbf{h})
is similar and hence skipped. Finally we show the tightness of
\accentset{\approx}{\mathbb{Q}}_{n,23}(\mathbf{h})=\accentset{\approx}{\mathbb{Q}}_{n,2}(\mathbf{h})+\accentset{\approx}{\mathbb{Q}}_{n,3}(\mathbf{h})
. As these terms only depend on
h_{3}
which has cadlag paths, we use equation (13.14) of Theorem 13.5 from
[
7
]
with
\beta=1/2
,
\alpha=1
and
F(x)=Cx
for some constant
C
. Fix
h_{3,1}<0<h_{3,2}<h_{3,3}
. The other cases (i.e. say
0<h_{3,1}<h_{3,2}<h_{3,3}
) are similar and hence skipped.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\accentset{\approx}{\mathbb{Q}}_{n,23}(h_{3,1})-\accentset{\approx}{\mathbb{Q}}_{n,23}(h_{3,2})\right|\left|\accentset{\approx}{\mathbb{Q}}_{n,23}(h_{3,2})-\accentset{\approx}{\mathbb{Q}}_{n,23}(h_{3,3})\right|\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+(\alpha_{0}-\beta_{0})\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi_{i})\right)\left[\mathds{1}_{d_{0}+\frac{h_{3,1}}{n}\leq X_{i}<d_{0}}-\mathds{1}_{d_{0}\leq X_{i}<d_{0}+\frac{h_{3,2}}{n}}\right]\right|\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\left.\times\left|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+(\alpha_{0}-\beta_{0})\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi_{i})\right)\left[\mathds{1}_{d_{0}\leq X_{i}<d_{0}+\frac{h_{3,2}}{n}}-\mathds{1}_{d_{0}\leq X_{i}<d_{0}+\frac{h_{3,3}}{n}}\right]\right|\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left|\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+(\alpha_{0}-\beta_{0})\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi_{i})\right|\left[\mathds{1}_{d_{0}+\frac{h_{3,1}}{n}\leq X_{i}<d_{0}}+\mathds{1}_{d_{0}\leq X_{i}<d_{0}+\frac{h_{3,2}}{n}}\right]\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\left.\times\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left|\tilde{H}_{k}\left(\xi_{i}+(\alpha_{0}-\beta_{0})\right)-\tilde{H}_{k}(\xi_{i})\right|\left[\mathds{1}_{d_{0}\leq X_{i}<d_{0}+\frac{h_{3,2}}{n}}+\mathds{1}_{d_{0}\leq X_{i}<d_{0}+\frac{h_{3,3}}{n}}\right]\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{k+1}{k}\left(4(\alpha_{0}-\beta_{0})k+\frac{1}{2}(\alpha_{0}-\beta_{0})^{2}+\left((\alpha_{0}-\beta_{0})\wedge 2k\right)^{2}\right)\times

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathds{1}_{d_{0}+\frac{h_{3,1}}{\sqrt{n}}\leq X_{i}<d_{0}+\frac{h_{3,2}}{n}}\right)\times\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathds{1}_{d_{0}+\frac{h_{3,2}}{n}\leq X_{i}<d_{0}+\frac{h_{3,3}}{n}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=C_{k,\theta_{0}}\sum_{i\neq j}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathds{1}_{d_{0}+\frac{h_{3,1}}{n}\leq X_{i}<d_{0}+\frac{h_{3,2}}{n}}\times\mathds{1}_{d_{0}+\frac{h_{3,2}}{n}\leq X_{j}<d_{0}+\frac{h_{3,3}}{n}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq C_{k,\theta_{0}}\times n^{2}\times\mathbb{P}\left(d_{0}+\frac{h_{3,1}}{n}\leq X<d_{0}+\frac{h_{3,2}}{n}\right)\times\mathbb{P}\left(d_{0}+\frac{h_{3,2}}{n}\leq X<d_{0}+\frac{h_{3,3}}{n}\right)

\displaystyle\leq C_{k,\theta_{0}}C^{2}\times(h_{3,2}-h_{3,1})\times(h_{3,3}-h_{3,2})\hskip 14.45377pt[C=\max_{x}f_{X}(x)]

\displaystyle\leq C_{k,\theta_{0}}C^{2}\times(h_{3,3}-h_{3,1})^{2}

This completes the proof of tightness of
\accentset{\approx}{\mathbb{Q}}_{n}
. Hence using equation (
A.30
) we conclude:

\accentset{\approx}{\mathbb{Q}}_{n}|_{\mathbb{I}}\overset{\mathscr{L}}{\implies}\mathbb{Q}|_{\mathbb{I}}\,.

which, along with equation (
A.29
) implies:

\tilde{\mathbb{Q}}_{n}|_{\mathbb{I}}\overset{\mathscr{L}}{\implies}\mathbb{Q}|_{\mathbb{I}}\,.

(A.31)

Finally, from the decomposition in equation (
A.16
) and combining our conclusions from equation (
A.17
), (
A.24
) and equation (
A.31
) we have:

\mathbb{Q}_{n}|_{\mathbb{I}}\overset{\mathscr{L}}{\implies}\mathbb{Q}|_{\mathbb{I}}\,.

(A.32)

Next and last step is to invoke argmin continuity mapping theorem to say that:

\widehat{\mathbf{h}}_{n}=\left(\sqrt{n}\left(\hat{\alpha}-\alpha_{0}\right),\sqrt{n}\left(\hat{\beta}-\beta_{0}\right),n\left(\hat{d}-d_{0}\right)\right)\overset{\mathscr{L}}{\implies}\text{mid argmin}_{\mathbf{h}\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbb{Q}(\mathbf{h})\,.

This will complete the proof. Following the proof of Lemma 3.2 of
[
20
]
, all we need to establish the joint asymptotic tightness of
\left\{\left(\mathbb{Q}_{n}(\mathbf{h}),\mathbb{J}_{n}(\mathbf{h})\right)\right\}_{n\in\mathbb{N}}
where
\mathbb{J}_{n}(\mathbf{h})
is the jump process corresponding to
\mathbb{Q}_{n}(\mathbf{h})
, i.e.

\mathbb{J}_{n}(\mathbf{h})=\text{sign}(h_{3})\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left[\mathds{1}_{X_{i}\leq d_{0}+\frac{h_{3}}{n}}-\mathds{1}_{X_{i}\leq d_{0}}\right]\,.

As we have already established the tightness of
\{\mathbb{Q}_{n}(\mathbf{h})\}
, we only need to establish the tightness of
\{\mathbb{J}_{n}(\mathbf{h})\}
. The proof is very similar to the proof of Lemma 3.2 of
[
20
]
and skipped here for the brevity.

A.6
Proofs of Theorem
2.2
and
2.3

The proofs of Theorem
2.2
and
2.3
are similar to that of Theorem
2.1
by replacing
\tilde{H}_{k}
with
\ell_{1}
loss function and
\ell_{2}
loss function respectively and hence skipped.

A.7
Proof of Theorem
3.7

Here we assume
\alpha_{0}=0,\beta_{0}=1
is known and derive the bound on the
\hat{d}
. When
\alpha_{0},\beta_{0}
is not known, then the problem becomes harder and rate of convergence obviously can not be faster. Our proof is based on the proof of Theorem 5 of
[
23
]
. Consider
\mathscr{A}
to be the set of all half-spaces, i.e.

\mathscr{A}=\left\{x^{\top}d>0\right\}_{d\in S^{p-1}}\,.

Our model is
X\sim P
and:

Y=\mathds{1}_{X^{\top}d>0}+\xi

where
\xi\sim\mathcal{N}(0,1)
. Now the class of hyperplanes
\mathscr{A}
has VC dimension
p
. From the properties of the hyperplane we know that, given any
N>p
(not to be confused with sample size
n
) there exist
x_{1},\dots,x_{N}\in\mathbb{R}^{p}
such that
\mathscr{A}
shatters all subsets of
\left\{x_{1},\dots,x_{N}\right\}
with cardinality
k\leq\lfloor p/2\rfloor:=V
(e.g. see
[
12
]
). Define
\Theta_{N,V}
to be the collection of all such
d\in S^{p-1}
which shatters all subsets of length
V
of
\left\{x_{1},\dots,x_{N}\right\}
. Hence we have:

\left\{\left(\mathds{1}_{x_{1}^{\top}d>0},\dots,\mathds{1}_{x_{N}^{\top}d>0}\right)\right\}_{d\in\Theta_{N,V}}:=B

where:

B=\left\{0,1\right\}_{N,V}=\left\{b\in\{0,1\}^{N}:\sum_{i=1}^{N}b_{i}=V\right\}\,.

Henceforth for any
d\in\Theta_{N,V}
we denote by
b_{d}
to be the corresponding unique
b\in B
. Define
\mu
to be the uniform measure on
\{x_{1},\dots,x_{N}\}
and for any
d\in\Theta_{N,V}
define:

Y=\mathds{1}_{X^{\top}d>0}+\xi\,.

The loss function we use here is the squared error loss defined as:

\displaystyle\ell\left(d,d_{0}\right)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[(Y-\mathds{1}_{X^{\top}d>0})^{2}-(Y-\mathds{1}_{X^{\top}d_{0}>0})^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{X}\left[\left|\mathds{1}_{X^{\top}d>0}-\mathds{1}_{X^{\top}d_{0}>0}\right|\right]

\displaystyle=\left\|\mathds{1}_{X^{\top}d>0}-\mathds{1}_{X^{\top}d_{0}>0}\right\|_{L_{1}(P)}\,.

The minimax risk is defined as:

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{n}

\displaystyle=\inf_{\hat{d}}\sup_{d\in S^{p-1}}\mathbb{E}_{d}\left[\ell(\hat{d},d)\right]

\displaystyle\geq\inf_{\hat{d}}\sup_{d\in\Theta_{N,V}}\mathbb{E}_{d}\left[\ell(\hat{d},d)\right]

\displaystyle=\inf_{\hat{d}}\sup_{d\in\Theta_{N,V}}\mathbb{E}_{d}\left[\left\|\mathds{1}_{X^{\top}\hat{d}>0}-\mathds{1}_{X^{\top}d>0}\right\|_{L_{1}(\mu)}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N}\inf_{\hat{d}}\sup_{d\in\Theta_{N,V}}\mathbb{E}_{d}\left[\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left|\mathds{1}_{x_{i}^{\top}\hat{d}>0}-\mathds{1}_{x_{i}^{\top}d>0}\right|_{L_{1}(\mu)}\right]

(A.33)

Now for any
\hat{d}\in S^{p-1}
, define
\hat{d}_{\text{new}}
as:

\hat{d}_{\text{new}}={\arg\min}_{d_{*}\in\Theta_{N,V}}\left\|\mathds{1}_{X^{\top}\hat{d}>0}-\mathds{1}_{X^{\top}d_{*}>0}\right\|_{L_{1}(\mu)}\,.

Then we have for any
d\in\Theta_{N,V}
:

\displaystyle\left\|\mathds{1}_{X^{\top}\hat{d}_{\text{new}}>0}-\mathds{1}_{X^{\top}d>0}\right\|_{L_{1}(\mu)}

\displaystyle=\left\|\mathds{1}_{X^{\top}\hat{d}_{\text{new}}>0}-\mathds{1}_{X^{\top}\hat{d}>0}+\mathds{1}_{X^{\top}\hat{d}>0}-\mathds{1}_{X^{\top}d>0}\right\|_{L_{1}(\mu)}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\mathds{1}_{X^{\top}\hat{d}_{\text{new}}>0}-\mathds{1}_{X^{\top}\hat{d}>0}\right\|_{L_{1}(\mu)}+\left\|\mathds{1}_{X^{\top}\hat{d}>0}-\mathds{1}_{X^{\top}d>0}\right\|_{L_{1}(\mu)}

\displaystyle\leq 2\left\|\mathds{1}_{X^{\top}\hat{d}>0}-\mathds{1}_{X^{\top}d>0}\right\|_{L_{1}(\mu)}\,.

Putting this bound in equation (
A.33
) we obtain:

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{n}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{N}\inf_{\hat{d}}\sup_{d\in\Theta_{N,V}}\mathbb{E}_{d}\left[\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left|\mathds{1}_{x_{i}^{\top}\hat{d}>0}-\mathds{1}_{x_{i}^{\top}d>0}\right|_{L_{1}(\mu)}\right]

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{2N}\inf_{\hat{d}}\sup_{d\in\Theta_{N,V}}\mathbb{E}_{d}\left[\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left|\mathds{1}_{x_{i}^{\top}\hat{d}_{\text{new}}>0}-\mathds{1}_{x_{i}^{\top}d>0}\right|_{L_{1}(\mu)}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2N}\inf_{\hat{d}\in\Theta_{N,V}}\sup_{d\in\Theta_{N,V}}\mathbb{E}_{d}\left[\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left|\mathds{1}_{x_{i}^{\top}\hat{d}>0}-\mathds{1}_{x_{i}^{\top}d>0}\right|_{L_{1}(\mu)}\right]

(A.34)

Next note that:

\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left|\mathds{1}_{x_{i}^{\top}\hat{d}>0}-\mathds{1}_{x_{i}^{\top}d>0}\right|=d_{H}\left(b_{\hat{d}},b_{d}\right)

where
d_{H}
is the Hamming distance. As
\Theta_{N,V}
has a bijection with
B
we can write equation (
A.34
) as:
